<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48968606' data-tags='Show HN,Hardware,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48968606' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(1) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48968606' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: I replaced a $120k bowling center system with $1,600 in ESP32s</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：用 1,600 美元的 ESP32 替代 12 万美元的保龄球馆系统</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48968606' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 2881</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48968606' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 343</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48968606'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An engineer documents swapping a $120k bowling center control system for an ESP32-based setup costing about $1.6k. The project demonstrates how commodity microcontrollers and open-source software can replace expensive, vendor-locked solutions in real-world venues. It also invites evaluation of trade-offs around reliability, maintenance, and support in commercial deployments.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一位工程师记录用约 1600 美元的 ESP32 方案替代原本价值约 12 万美元的保龄球馆控制系统。此项目展示了商用场景中，普通微控制器与开源软件仍有能力替代昂贵的、厂商绑定的解决方案。亦需权衡可靠性、维护与售后支持等现实挑战。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://news.ycombinator.com/y18.svg' data-full='https://news.ycombinator.com/y18.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48734373' data-tags='AI,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48734373' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(2) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://thereallo.dev/blog/claude-code-prompt-steganography' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Claude Code is steganographically marking requests</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Claude Code 以隐写方式标记请求</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48734373' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 2395</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48734373' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 731</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48734373'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers demonstrate how Claude Code can embed hidden signals in prompts, enabling steganographic tagging of requests. This raises questions about model privacy, misuse, and provenance in AI-assisted workflows. The finding highlights the need for robust controls around prompt encoding.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究者展示了 Claude Code 如何在提示中嵌入隐写信号，给请求标记隐藏标识。这引发对模型隐私、滥用和工作流可追溯性的担忧，凸显了对提示编码的鲁棒控制的必要性。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

It emphasizes agent-driven workflows and scalable data access, signaling a push toward more transparent, configurable intelligence tooling.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Kimi 发表 K3，作为一个开放前沿情报平台，概述了其架构与在开放数据探索中的潜在应用场景。强调代理驱动的工作流与可扩展的数据访问，预示向更透明、可配置的情报工具迈进。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(4) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.anthropic.com/news/claude-opus-5' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Claude Opus 5</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Claude Opus 5 发布</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49038433' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1754</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49038433' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1296</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49038433'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Anthropic unveils Claude Opus 5, the latest model in the Claude Opus line. It marks another step in upgrading instruction-following, safety controls, and performance for enterprise-grade AI assistants.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Anthropic 发布 Claude Opus 5，Claude Opus 系列的最新版本，进一步提升了指令遵循、安全控制和企业级 AI 助手的性能。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48755965' data-tags='Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48755965' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(5) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://f-droid.org/2026/07/01/adv-malware.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Android Developer Verification: Threat masquerading as protection</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Android 开发者验证：伪装成保护的威胁</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48755965' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1665</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48755965' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 714</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48755965'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Android developer verification can be abused by threat actors who disguise malware as protective or security tools. Such tactics exploit trust signals around security features to mislead users or bypass vetting, undermining platform defenses. The takeaway is a push for tighter vetting, clearer indicators of app behavior, and user skepticism when apps claim to protect you.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Android 开发者验证可能被威胁者利用，伪装成保护性工具的恶意软件。此类手段利用安全功能的信任信号误导用户或规避审核，削弱平台防护。要点在于加强审核、提供更清晰的应用行为指示，并提高用户在遇到自称保护工具的应用时的警惕。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://f-droid.org/assets/fdroid-logo_bfHl7nsLHOUQxzdU8-rGIhn4bAgl6z7k2mA3fWoCyT4=.png' data-full='https://f-droid.org/assets/fdroid-logo_bfHl7nsLHOUQxzdU8-rGIhn4bAgl6z7k2mA3fWoCyT4=.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48865019' data-tags='Legal,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48865019' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(6) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://9to5mac.com/2026/07/10/apple-sues-openai-trade-secret-theft/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Apple sues OpenAI, accuses ex-employees of stealing trade secrets</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>苹果控告 OpenAI，指控前员工窃取商业机密</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48865019' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1612</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48865019' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 920</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48865019'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A high-profile lawsuit pits Apple against OpenAI over alleged misappropriation of trade secrets by former OpenAI staff. The case centers on proprietary AI techniques and internal data siphoned during a transition, signaling growing tension as big tech fights over AI talent. The outcome could influence how firms protect confidential knowledge during acquisitions and departures.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一起备受关注的诉讼指控苹果指控OpenAI及其前员工窃取商业机密，聚焦于AI技术与内部数据的可能外泄。此案凸显大型科技公司在人才流动与知识保护方面的博弈，将影响企业在并购和员工离职时的机密信息防护策略。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i0.wp.com/9to5mac.com/wp-content/uploads/sites/6/2026/05/apple-openai.jpg?resize=1200%2C628&amp;quality=82&amp;strip=all&amp;ssl=1' data-full='https://i0.wp.com/9to5mac.com/wp-content/uploads/sites/6/2026/05/apple-openai.jpg?resize=1200%2C628&amp;quality=82&amp;strip=all&amp;ssl=1' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48843923' data-tags='Politics,Privacy,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48843923' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(7) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.patrick-breyer.de/en/eu-parliament-greenlights-chat-control-1-0-breyer-our-children-lose-out/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>EU Parliament greenlights Chat Control 1.0</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>欧盟议会通过聊天监控1.0，Breyer：我们的孩子要吃亏</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48843923' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1603</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48843923' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 836</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48843923'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>This marks a major push to enable cross-border scanning of encrypted messages in pursuit of child safety and crime prevention. Critics warn that the measure expands surveillance at the expense of privacy and free expression, while Breyer argues that children will lose out.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这标志着推动跨境扫描加密通讯以提升儿童安全和打击犯罪的重大举措。批评者担心此举侵蚀隐私与言论自由，而Breyer则认为这会让儿童吃亏。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.patrick-breyer.de/wp-content/uploads/2023/07/logo_regular_vertical.png' data-full='https://www.patrick-breyer.de/wp-content/uploads/2023/07/logo_regular_vertical.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48997548' data-tags='Security,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48997548' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(8) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://openai.com/index/hugging-face-model-evaluation-security-incident/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>OpenAI and Hugging Face address security incident during model evaluation</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>OpenAI 与 Hugging Face 解决模型评估中的安全事件</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48997548' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1590</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48997548' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1110</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48997548'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI and Hugging Face coordinated after a security incident affecting model evaluation across their platforms. They outline the investigation steps, remediation actions, and ongoing safeguards to prevent data leaks and misuse during evaluation. The episode underscores the fragility of cross-platform evaluation pipelines and the need for hardened security controls in AI collaboration.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI 与 Hugging Face 在一次影响模型评估的安全事件后开展协作，披露调查进展、整改措施及持续的安全防护。此次事件凸显跨平台评估流程的脆弱性，以及在 AI 领域加强安全控制的重要性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://images.ctfassets.net/kftzwdyauwt9/1kXZRSOAQbv2BDKEO14tJl/b2f0a336afaddc32e5776ba555bc4dbf/Hugging_Face_Security_Incident_16x9.png?w=1600&amp;h=900&amp;fit=fill' data-full='https://images.ctfassets.net/kftzwdyauwt9/1kXZRSOAQbv2BDKEO14tJl/b2f0a336afaddc32e5776ba555bc4dbf/Hugging_Face_Security_Incident_16x9.png?w=1600&amp;h=900&amp;fit=fill' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(9) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://openai.com/index/gpt-5-6/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>GPT-5.6</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>GPT-5.6 版本</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48849066' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1531</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48849066' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1087</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A new GPT-5.6 model appears, signaling continued iteration on the GPT-5 family. Expect refinements in reasoning, safety, and efficiency, reflecting the ongoing cadence of large language model updates.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>GPT-5.6 版正逐步进入，表示 GPT-5 系列的持续迭代。预计在推理、安全与效率方面有改进，体现大语言模型更新的持续节奏。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48889637' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48889637' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(10) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://raymyers.org/post/zed-creator-calls-spade-a-spade/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Zig Creator Calls Spade a Spade, Anthropic Blows Smoke</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Zig 创作者直言不讳，Anthropic 敷衍其词</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48889637' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1493</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48889637' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 761</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48889637'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An outspoken Zig language creator takes a blunt stance on AI safety claims associated with Anthropic, challenging the industry’s framing and transparency. The controversy underscores the tension between ambitious safety promises and practical concerns from developers who build on AI tools. It highlights how independent voices can shape public scrutiny of AI vendors.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Zig 语言的创作者对 Anthropic 的 AI 安全说法发表直言，挑战行业的表述与透明度。此事凸显了安全承诺的宏大叙事与开发者对 AI 工具的实际关切之间的张力。独立声音可能影响对 AI 供应商的公众监督。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://raymyers.org/weblog-card-1.png' data-full='https://raymyers.org/weblog-card-1.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49022152' data-tags='Education,Health'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49022152' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(11) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://nealstephenson.substack.com/p/writing-by-hand-is-good-for-your' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Writing by hand is good for your brain</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>手写对大脑有益</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49022152' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1455</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49022152' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 655</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49022152'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Handwriting engages motor memory and deeper processing, often boosting recall more than typing for learning tasks. The act of forming letters seems to strengthen neural connections that support long-term memory and understanding. For students and knowledge workers, mixing handwritten notes with digital tools can improve retention.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>手写能激活运动记忆并促使更深入的信息处理，常常比打字在学习任务中带来更高的记忆保留。手写过程有助于强化支持长期记忆和理解的神经连接。对于学生和知识工作者，混合使用手写笔记与数字工具有利于提升记忆留存。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48829312' data-tags='Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48829312' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(12) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://tris.sherliker.net/blog/obfuscated-self-evaluating-bash-script-by-cdn-akamai-being-supplied-to-consumers-via-retail-stores/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Decoding the obfuscated bash script on a Uniqlo t-shirt</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>解码优衣库 T 恤上的混淆 Bash 脚本</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48829312' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1416</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48829312' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 226</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48829312'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers embedded an obfuscated, self-evaluating Bash script on a Uniqlo T-shirt delivered through a CDN. The stunt demonstrates how software-like code can slip into consumer channels via retail distribution, highlighting trust and supply-chain risks. It argues for stronger code provenance checks and verification in modern supply networks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究人员在优衣库 T 恤上嵌入一个混淆、可自我评估的 Bash 脚本，并通过 CDN 交付给消费者。这一举动揭示了软件代码通过零售渠道渗透到消费端的风险与供应链信任问题，并呼吁在现代供应链中加强代码来源核验。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://tris.sherliker.net/assets/name.png' data-full='https://tris.sherliker.net/assets/name.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49124218'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Elevators reveal how urban infrastructure adapts to growing vertical cities, highlighting safety, efficiency, and automation trends.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>电梯揭示了城市基础设施如何适应日益垂直化的城市，突出安全、效率与自动化趋势。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(14) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://huggingface.co/moonshotai/Kimi-K3' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Kimi-K3 on HuggingFace</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Kimi-K3 在 HuggingFace 上</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49065752' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1355</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49065752' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 536</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49065752'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Kimi-K3 is now available on HuggingFace, enabling researchers and developers to try, fine-tune, and benchmark the model with familiar tooling. The release underscores the ongoing push to open-source options and community collaboration around language models on popular platforms.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Kimi-K3 已在 HuggingFace 上公开，可供研究者和开发者直接试用与微调。此举进一步推动开源模型在 HuggingFace 生态中的扩展，便于社区贡献和基准测试。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn-thumbnails.huggingface.co/social-thumbnails/models/moonshotai/Kimi-K3.png' data-full='https://cdn-thumbnails.huggingface.co/social-thumbnails/models/moonshotai/Kimi-K3.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48838876' data-tags='Legal,Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48838876' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(15) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://apnews.com/article/john-deere-right-to-repair-agriculture-equipment-cb7514ffedb95c130a976af661f2bc02' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>John Deere owners will get the right to repair equipment under FTC settlement</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>FTC和解赋予约翰迪尔设备的修理权</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48838876' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1355</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48838876' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 294</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48838876'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An FTC settlement grants John Deere equipment owners the right to repair, potentially lowering downtime and repair costs. It signals a shift toward repairability by nudging OEMs to share tools, manuals, and parts, and could boost independent repair shops. This case could influence broader right-to-repair momentum in tech-enabled farming.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>FTC与约翰迪尔设备修理权的和解，可能降低停机时间与维修成本，推动制造商提供维修工具、手册与零件。此举或促成独立修理商的兴起，或对农业科技领域的修理权运动产生示范效应。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://dims.apnews.com/dims4/default/eb2d449/2147483647/strip/true/crop/4513x3007+0+162/resize/980x653!/quality/90/?url=https%3A%2F%2Fassets.apnews.com%2Fca%2Fc8%2F5fff3b3e7c95ef4bbd4696383598%2F674e975157b745d28967fa953a18c3d4' data-full='https://dims.apnews.com/dims4/default/eb2d449/2147483647/strip/true/crop/4513x3007+0+162/resize/980x653!/quality/90/?url=https%3A%2F%2Fassets.apnews.com%2Fca%2Fc8%2F5fff3b3e7c95ef4bbd4696383598%2F674e975157b745d28967fa953a18c3d4' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>半成品化的产品</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48772388' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1297</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48772388' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 387</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48772388'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A critique of launching products without proper UX and polish, offering guidance on aligning product scope with real user needs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>该文批评匆忙推出未充分打磨的产品，提出将产品范围与真实用户需求对齐的建议。</p>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49063022' data-tags='Privacy,Legal'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49063022' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(17) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.techspot.com/news/113236-us-prosecutors-charge-atlanta-man-after-grapheneos-phone.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>US citizen charged after GrapheneOS phone wipes during airport search</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>美国公民在机场安检时因 GrapheneOS 自动清除数据而被起诉</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49063022' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1297</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49063022' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1050</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49063022'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The case highlights how privacy-focused devices like GrapheneOS can trigger data wipe during border checks, leading to legal action. It underscores tensions between personal data protection, law enforcement, and the risk of unintended data loss.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一起案例显示隐私导向设备在机场安检时可能因安全特性触发数据擦除，进而引发起诉。凸显了个人数据保护、执法权力与意外数据丢失之间的张力。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.techspot.com/images2/news/bigimage/2026/07/2026-07-26-image-4.jpg' data-full='https://www.techspot.com/images2/news/bigimage/2026/07/2026-07-26-image-4.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48945241' data-tags='Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48945241' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(18) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48945241' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AWS: Inaccurate Estimated Billing Data – $1.7 billion</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AWS：估算账单数据不准确，涉额17亿美元</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48945241' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1276</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48945241' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 744</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48945241'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Amazon Web Services disclosed inaccuracies in its estimated billing data, leading to charges totaling about $1.7B across customers. The incident highlights how cloud-cost visibility can fail at scale and underscores the need for robust auditing, refund mechanisms, and better tooling for cost governance in large cloud environments.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>云成本监控在大规模云环境中暴露出不足，估算账单数据存在不准确之处，涉案金额约17亿美元。此事凸显需要更强的成本可视化、审计与退款流程，并推动改进的成本管理工具与治理方法。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://news.ycombinator.com/y18.svg' data-full='https://news.ycombinator.com/y18.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48736605' data-tags='AI,Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48736605' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(19) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.anthropic.com/news/claude-sonnet-5' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Claude Sonnet 5</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Claude Sonnet 5</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48736605' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1248</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48736605' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 773</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48736605'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Anthropic released Claude Sonnet 5, the newest member of its Claude family. The update emphasizes stronger reasoning capabilities and tighter safety controls for enterprise AI deployments.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Anthropic 发布 Claude Sonnet 5，这是 Claude 家族的最新成员，强调改进的推理能力与企业部署的安全控制。它面向企业场景，优化推理质量并提升对安全约束的可控性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.sanity.io/images/4zrzovbb/website/458ea645ef6b729f6847cba16932716e6b547f2f-2880x1620.png' data-full='https://cdn.sanity.io/images/4zrzovbb/website/458ea645ef6b729f6847cba16932716e6b547f2f-2880x1620.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48979269' data-tags='AI,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48979269' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(20) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://werd.io/american-ai-is-locked-down-and-proprietary-its-losing/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>China’s open-weights AI strategy is winning</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>中国的开放权重AI策略正在取得胜利</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48979269' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1226</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48979269' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 925</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48979269'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>China is pushing an open-weights AI strategy—favoring open-source models, shared datasets, and domestic collaboration. This approach is gaining momentum and challenging the dominance of proprietary AI, potentially accelerating adoption across sectors and reshaping global AI competition.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>中国正在推动开放权重的 AI 策略，重点支持开源模型、共用数据集与国内生态协作。这一路径正在获得势头，挑战以往的专有 AI 主导地位，或将加速各行业的应用并改变全球 AI 竞争格局。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1548192746-dd526f154ed9?crop=entropy&amp;cs=tinysrgb&amp;fit=max&amp;fm=jpg&amp;ixid=M3wxMTc3M3wwfDF8c2VhcmNofDJ8fGluZGllfGVufDB8fHx8MTc0OTY5ODcxM3ww&amp;ixlib=rb-4.1.0&amp;q=80&amp;w=2000' data-full='https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1548192746-dd526f154ed9?crop=entropy&amp;cs=tinysrgb&amp;fit=max&amp;fm=jpg&amp;ixid=M3wxMTc3M3wwfDF8c2VhcmNofDJ8fGluZGllfGVufDB8fHx8MTc0OTY5ODcxM3ww&amp;ixlib=rb-4.1.0&amp;q=80&amp;w=2000' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49113929' data-tags='Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49113929' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(21) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.uefa.com/news-media/news/02a7-213a92896eb0-54dfbf454e3b-1000--statement-on-behalf-of-uefa-and-its-55-national-associations/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>UEFA and its national associations will not participate in FIFA competitions</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>欧足联及55个成员协会宣布退出FIFA赛事</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49113929' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1214</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49113929' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 665</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49113929'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>UEFA and 55 national associations announced they will not participate in FIFA competitions, signaling a governance dispute between regional bodies and FIFA. The move could disrupt the international calendar and pressure FIFA to concede on reform terms.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>欧洲足联与55个成员协会宣布不参与FIFA赛事，标志着与FIFA在治理与日程安排上的公开分歧。此举可能扰乱国际日历，促使对改革条款的谈判加剧。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://editorial.uefa.com/resources/02a7-213acb6c89f6-6c8e1ae54e77-1000/uefa_corporate_logo_all_crops.png' data-full='https://editorial.uefa.com/resources/02a7-213acb6c89f6-6c8e1ae54e77-1000/uefa_corporate_logo_all_crops.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48924912' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(22) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://thinkingmachines.ai/news/introducing-inkling/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Inkling: Our Open-Weights Model</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Inkling：我们的开源权重模型</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48924912' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1196</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48924912' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 285</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48924912'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Inkling is Thinking Machines&#x27; open-weights model. It signals a broader push toward transparent, testable architectures. Open weights enable researchers to compare methods and stress-test safety features, potentially accelerating progress while requiring thoughtful governance.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Inkling 是 Thinking Machines 的开源权重模型。这体现了对透明、可测试架构的推进。开源权重便于研究者对比方法、测试安全性，但也需要谨慎治理以防滥用。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49076057' data-tags='AI,Open Source,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49076057' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(23) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.anthropic.com/news/position-open-weights-models' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Our position on open-weights models</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>关于开放权重模型的立场</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49076057' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1161</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49076057' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1713</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49076057'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Anthropic outlines its stance on open-weights models, emphasizing safety, governance, and responsible sharing over unrestricted release. It discusses the risk of misuse and proposes criteria for when and how weights should be shared or accessed.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Anthropic 阐述对开放权重模型的立场，强调在安全、治理与负责任共享之间取得平衡，而非盲目公开。文中指出若在缺乏防护的情况下公开权重可能被滥用，并提出负责任共享与准入控制的标准。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.sanity.io/images/4zrzovbb/website/62993bb857986e2808c2a75024f384e7b85312b6-2400x1254.png' data-full='https://cdn.sanity.io/images/4zrzovbb/website/62993bb857986e2808c2a75024f384e7b85312b6-2400x1254.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48956688' data-tags='Privacy,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48956688' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(25) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://videocardz.com/newz/lg-monitors-silently-install-software-through-windows-update-without-user-consent' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>LG monitors silently install software through Windows Update without consent</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>LG显示器通过Windows更新在未征得同意的情况下悄悄安装软件</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48956688' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1148</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48956688' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 581</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48956688'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>LG monitors were found to silently install companion software through Windows Update without explicit user consent. The bundled software can run in the background and may raise privacy and security concerns, creating a potential for bloat. Readers should review update offers, remove unwanted components, and consider blocking vendor software when possible.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究发现，LG显示器通过Windows Update在未明确同意的情况下安装附带软件。该软件可能后台运行，带来隐私与安全风险，并可能造成系统臃肿。读者应在更新时谨慎勾选、移除不需要的组件，并在可行时阻止厂商软件推送。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.videocardz.com/1/2026/07/LG-MONITORS-MCAFFE.jpg' data-full='https://cdn.videocardz.com/1/2026/07/LG-MONITORS-MCAFFE.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48797916' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(26) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.opentools.studio/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>OpenPrinter</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>OpenPrinter：开放打印机项目</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48797916' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1123</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48797916' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 283</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48797916'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>OpenPrinter describes an open-source printing project with modular firmware and hardware design, aiming to reduce vendor lock-in and empower communities to customize printers. The initiative could accelerate repairability, transparency, and ecosystem innovation for makers and businesses alike.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>OpenPrinter 是一个开放打印机项目，强调可模块化固件与硬件设计，旨在降低厂商锁定并让社区参与定制。该方向可能促进可修复性、透明度和生态创新，但也需关注可持续性与供应链问题。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Show HN: 18 Words 展示了一个极简写作工具，帖子被限制为18个单词，强调简洁与快速分享。此举体现了通过约束提升表达清晰度、降低噪声的设计理念。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(28) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://chatgpt.com/share/6a5fdc7a-d6f8-83e8-bbea-8deb42cfed56' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Terence Tao&#x27;s ChatGPT conversation about the Jacobian Conjecture counterexample</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>陶哲軒與 ChatGPT 的 Jacobian 猜想反例對話</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49010345' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1105</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49010345' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 628</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49010345'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Terrence Tao engages ChatGPT in a discussion about the Jacobian Conjecture, exploring a potential counterexample and the AI&#x27;s reasoning limits. The exchange highlights both the promise and the pitfalls of using language models for advanced mathematical problem-solving.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>宇宙数学大师泰罗（Terrence Tao）与 ChatGPT 展开对话，探讨雅可比猜想的潜在反例及 AI 推理的局限性。此互动凸显了将大型语言模型用于高层次数学推理的前景与风险。</p>

</div>

</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI opens a new advertising option within ChatGPT, letting brands reach engaged users in chat sessions. This monetization move could reshape user experience and ad-tech expectations as conversational interfaces become a media channel.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI 在 ChatGPT 内开放广告位，让品牌在对话中触达用户。这一举措将影响用户体验与广告生态，改变以对话为媒介的变现与投放策略。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49023016' data-tags='Startups,AI,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49023016' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(32) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.politico.com/news/2026/07/22/startup-founders-urge-trump-not-to-shut-off-chinese-open-weight-ai-01008992' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Startup founders urge U.S. government not to shut off Chinese open weight AI</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>创业者敦促特朗普不要切断对中国开源权重 AI 的访问</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49023016' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1052</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49023016' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 868</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49023016'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Founders argue that cutting off access to Chinese open-weight AI would throttle innovation, raise costs, and fragment global collaboration. They call for governance and standards instead of broad bans, highlighting that open weights accelerate safety research and tooling when responsibly managed.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>创始人认为切断中国开源权重 AI 的访问会扼杀创新、提高成本，并分裂全球协作。他们呼吁以治理和标准取代全面禁令，强调在负责任管理下，开源权重能加速安全研究和工具开发。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48886741' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48886741' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(33) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48886741' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Ask HN: Add flag for AI-generated articles</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Ask HN：为 AI 生成的文章加上标记</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48886741' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1049</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48886741' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 449</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48886741'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A discussion about introducing a flag to label AI-generated content on HN. It weighs transparency, reader trust, and the potential impact on ranking, moderation, and author accountability.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>讨论在 Hacker News 上为 AI 生成的文章添加标记的可行性与影响。核心在于提升透明度与读者信任，同时考虑对排序、审核和作者责任的潜在影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://news.ycombinator.com/y18.svg' data-full='https://news.ycombinator.com/y18.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(34) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://bento.page/slides/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Bento - An entire PowerPoint in one HTML file (edit+view+data+collab)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN: Bento——把整份 PowerPoint 装进一个 HTML 文件（可编辑、查看、数据与协作）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49008211' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 999</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49008211' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 234</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49008211'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Bento packaging turns an entire PowerPoint into a single HTML file, with built-in editing, viewing, data integration, and collaboration. This approach enables portable, offline-friendly presentations and simplifies sharing.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Bento 将整份 PowerPoint 打包进一个 HTML 文件，具备编辑、查看、数据整合与协作功能。实现了演示文稿的便携、离线友好与简化分享。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49045159' data-tags='Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49045159' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(35) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://kitsumed.github.io/blog/posts/android-may-soon-restrict-on-device-adb/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Android may soon restrict on-device ADB</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Android 可能很快限制设备端 ADB</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49045159' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 955</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49045159' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 470</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49045159'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Google is tightening on-device ADB controls, requiring user authorization for new hosts and limiting access to trusted contexts. The move aims to shrink the attack surface of debugging tools on Android, but may complicate automation and enterprise workflows. Expect gradual rollout across upcoming Android updates.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Android 正在加强对设备端 ADB 的控制，要求对新主机进行授权并限制对受信任环境以外的访问。此举旨在降低调试工具的攻击面，但可能增加自动化与企业工作流的难度。相关改动将会在未来的 Android 更新中逐步落地。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://kitsumed.goatcounter.com/count?p=/blog/posts/android-may-soon-restrict-on-device-adb&amp;t=Android%20May%20Soon%20Restrict%20On-Device%20ADB%2c%20Affecting%20Shizuku%2c%20libadb%20and%20Developers' data-full='https://kitsumed.goatcounter.com/count?p=/blog/posts/android-may-soon-restrict-on-device-adb&amp;t=Android%20May%20Soon%20Restrict%20On-Device%20ADB%2c%20Affecting%20Shizuku%2c%20libadb%20and%20Developers' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48740771' data-tags='Legal,Business,AI,Security,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48740771' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(36) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://twitter.com/AnthropicAI/status/2072106151890809341' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Department of Commerce has lifted export controls on Claude Fable 5 and Mythos 5</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>商务部取消对 Claude Fable 5 与 Mythos 5 的出口管制</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48740771' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 946</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48740771' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 669</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48740771'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Department of Commerce has eased export controls on Claude Fable 5 and Mythos 5, enabling broader international distribution. The move could accelerate global AI deployment and competition, while inviting regulatory and security considerations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>商务部放松了对 Claude Fable 5 和 Mythos 5 的出口管制，使其在全球范围内的分发更为广泛。这一举措可能加速全球 AI 的部署与竞争，同时带来监管与安全方面的考量。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://pbs.twimg.com/profile_images/1798110641414443008/XP8gyBaY_200x200.jpg' data-full='https://pbs.twimg.com/profile_images/1798110641414443008/XP8gyBaY_200x200.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48767347' data-tags='Privacy,Legal,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48767347' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(37) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.hunton.com/privacy-and-cybersecurity-law-blog/virginia-bans-sale-of-geolocation-data' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Virginia bans sale of precise geolocation data</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>弗吉尼亚州禁止出售地理位置数据</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48767347' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 941</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48767347' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 137</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48767347'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Virginia&#x27;s new privacy rule bans the sale of geolocation data collected from devices. The move tightens location data markets and will impact marketers, data brokers, and app developers who rely on location signals. It also heightens regulatory fragmentation as more states consider similar restrictions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>弗吉尼亚州出台隐私新规，禁止出售设备收集的地理位置信息数据。此举收紧了地理位置信息市场，影响依赖位置信号的广告商、数据经纪人与应用开发者。同时，这也可能推动其他州采取类似限制，加剧合规碎片化。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.hunton.com/privacy-and-cybersecurity-law-blog/assets/images-t1783027116/206510.png' data-full='https://www.hunton.com/privacy-and-cybersecurity-law-blog/assets/images-t1783027116/206510.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48747304' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48747304' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(38) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.quantamagazine.org/for-the-first-time-a-cell-built-from-scratch-grows-and-divides-20260701/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>For first time, a cell built from scratch grows and divides</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>首次从零构建的细胞能够生长并分裂</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48747304' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 940</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48747304' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 295</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48747304'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers have built a cell from scratch that can grow and divide, marking a milestone in bottom-up synthetic biology. By assembling functional cellular life from individual components, the work advances the dream of programmable biology and could unlock new biotechnologies and origins-of-life insights. Yet, challenges around stability, safety, and control remain as researchers push toward more complex designs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究者首次从零构建出可生长和分裂的细胞，标志着自下而上的合成生物学里程碑。通过从单个组件组装功能性细胞生命，这项工作推动了可编程生物学的实现，并可能为生物技术和起源研究带来新机遇。当然，稳定性、安全性与可控性仍是未来进一步复杂化设计时需要面对的挑战。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.quantamagazine.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/SyntheticLife-cr-Ada-Zejun-Shen-social.jpg' data-full='https://www.quantamagazine.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/SyntheticLife-cr-Ada-Zejun-Shen-social.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49012070' data-tags='Culture,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49012070' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(39) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://twitter.com/na_announce/status/2079952538040672302' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>John C. Dvorak has died</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>约翰·C·德沃拉克去世</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49012070' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 920</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49012070' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 311</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49012070'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Veteran tech journalist John C. Dvorak has died, leaving a controversial but influential footprint in tech media and early PC culture. His outspoken opinions and long-running columns helped shape debates around computing hardware and industry trends.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>资深科技记者约翰·C·德沃拉克去世，他在科技媒体与早期个人电脑文化中留下了富有争议但具影响力的足迹。他直言不讳的观点与长篇专栏塑造了关于计算硬件与行业趋势的讨论。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://pbs.twimg.com/profile_images/1350980458/na_twitter2_200x200.gif' data-full='https://pbs.twimg.com/profile_images/1350980458/na_twitter2_200x200.gif' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48966120' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48966120' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(40) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://twitter.com/Alibaba_Qwen/status/2078759124914098291' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Qwen 3.8</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Qwen 3.8 版本</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48966120' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 917</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48966120' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 649</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48966120'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Alibaba&#x27;s Qwen 3.8 is the latest release in its Qwen family, aiming to improve instruction-following and response quality. The update underscores a continued push by Chinese tech firms to scale capable LLMs for both consumer apps and enterprise use. As with similar releases, safety, guardrails, and domain-specific tuning are likely key focus areas.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>阿里巴巴推出 Qwen 3.8，作为 Qwen 系列的最新版本，力求提升指令遵循能力与输出质量。此举反映中国厂商在大模型领域继续扩张的趋势，覆盖消费者应用与企业场景。与其他厂商的更新一样，安全性、约束机制和领域定制将成为核心关注点。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://pbs.twimg.com/media/HNk8XP_aoAAUShj.jpg:large' data-full='https://pbs.twimg.com/media/HNk8XP_aoAAUShj.jpg:large' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49063754' data-tags='Programming,Open Source,Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49063754' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(41) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://nikolays.github.io/PGSimCity/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>PGSimCity - How PostgreSQL Works</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>PGSimCity：PostgreSQL 的工作原理</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49063754' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 912</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49063754' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 89</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49063754'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>PGSimCity uses a city-building metaphor to illuminate PostgreSQL internals, from storage and indexing to the query planner and executor. It helps readers understand how MVCC, WAL, and background processes combine to make PostgreSQL reliable and scalable.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>PGSimCity 以城市场景比喻讲解 PostgreSQL 的内部结构，包括存储、索引、查询优化器和执行器。帮助读者理解 MVCC、WAL 等机制是如何协同工作，提升可靠性与可扩展性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://nikolays.github.io/PGSimCity/og.png' data-full='https://nikolays.github.io/PGSimCity/og.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48915709' data-tags='Hardware,Design'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48915709' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(42) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://fabiensanglard.net/jurrasic_park_computers/index.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Jurassic Park computers in excruciating detail</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>《侏罗纪公园》中的计算机：详尽到极致</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48915709' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 906</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48915709' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 241</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48915709'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>This piece dissects the film&#x27;s on-screen computer systems with an eye for hardware, OS choices, and faux interfaces. It reveals how the prop-makers staged computing to look convincing on 1993-era tech, shaping viewers&#x27; perception of digital authority. The analysis also explains why these UI aesthetics continue to influence cinematic design.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文逐步拆解《侏罗纪公园》中的银幕计算机，揭示道具团队如何选用真实硬件与界面语言来营造可信感，并分析这对当代电影界面设计的影响。作者还讨论其对后续影视作品在呈现技术方面的影响力。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://fabiensanglard.net/jurrasic_park_computers/00_11_15_Apple_Powerbook_100.webp' data-full='https://fabiensanglard.net/jurrasic_park_computers/00_11_15_Apple_Powerbook_100.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48818311' data-tags='Politics,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48818311' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(43) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://fightchatcontrol.eu/chat-control-overview' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Chat Control 1.0 and 2.0 Explained</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Chat Control 1.0 与 2.0 的解读</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48818311' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 900</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48818311' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 340</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48818311'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>This explainer breaks down the evolution of Chat Control policies across versions 1.0 and 2.0, focusing on moderation vs. innovation trade-offs and regulatory implications. It helps developers assess how such rules affect platform design and user safety.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>系统梳理了 Chat Control 1.0 与 2.0 的要点与演进，聚焦于监管与创新之间的权衡，以及对平台设计和用户安全的影响。为开发者评估相关政策的影响提供了清晰视角。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48842459' data-tags='Show HN,AI,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48842459' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(44) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/JustVugg/colibri' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Getting GLM 5.2 running on my slow computer</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：在慢速电脑上让 GLM 5.2 运行起来</p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>开发者记录在慢速计算机上运行 GLM 5.2 的过程，提供具体步骤、限制与调整方法。展示了通过轻量化推理路径和社区工具在中等硬件上也能获得可用性能的可能。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49098510' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(45) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/drumih/turbo-fieldfare' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Open-source engine running Gemma 4 26B in 2 GB RAM on any M-series Mac</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：在任意 M-series Mac 的 2GB RAM 上运行 Gemma 4 26B 的开源引擎</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49098510' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 896</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49098510' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 330</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49098510'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An open-source engine demonstrates running Gemma 4 26B with only 2 GB RAM on M-series Macs, using efficient quantization and memory management. This lowers the barrier to local LLM deployment and shows consumer hardware can still handle mid-size models with clever optimizations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个开源引擎在 M-series Mac 上仅用 2GB RAM 就能运行 Gemma 4 26B，依靠高效量化与内存管理。此举降低本地部署中等规模模型的门槛，显示消费级硬件通过优化仍具备可行性。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(46) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://fireworks.ai/blog/kimik3-fable' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Kimi K3 Is Competitive with Fable; Kimi K3 and Fable Is SoTA</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Kimi K3 与 Fable 比肩，达到顶尖水平</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48999291' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 863</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48999291' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 434</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48999291'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Kimi K3 delivers competitive performance against Fable, suggesting it has reached a state-of-the-art level in certain benchmarks. The claim underscores a rising parity among high-end open/white-box models, intensifying competition in the LLM space.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Kimi K3 的表现与 Fable 相当，在某些基准测试中已接近顶尖水平。此举反映出高端开源模型之间日益接近的实力，对 LLM 生态的竞争格局将产生重要影响。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(47) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://stratechery.com/2026/whos-afraid-of-chinese-models/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Who&#x27;s afraid of Chinese models?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>谁害怕中国模型？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48977128' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 853</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48977128' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 684</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48977128'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Stratechery argues that fear of Chinese AI models may be overstated and discusses geopolitical dynamics around model development, data, and competitiveness. It suggests Western firms should rethink dependency and collaboration strategies rather than succumb to alarm about Chinese tech.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Stratechery 指出对中国模型的恐惧或许被高估，分析了在模型开发、数据与竞争力方面的地缘政治动态。文章建议西方公司重新思考依赖与合作策略，而非对中国技术过度惊慌。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(48) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://christianselig.com/2026/07/vision-pro-house/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The coolest use for the Vision Pro</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49102774' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 823</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49102774' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 314</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49102774'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A hands-on look at turning Vision Pro into a home-control hub, overlaying dashboards and controls into your living space. The piece illustrates how AR can blend physical devices with digital control, hinting at future everyday use of consumer XR hardware.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>通过实际演示，揭示将 Vision Pro 打造成家庭控制中心的可能性——在现实空间中覆盖仪表板和设备控制。该探讨展示了增强现实如何把物理设备与数字控制融合在一起，预示着普通消费者对 XR 硬件的日常使用前景。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49033004' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(50) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://ptrchm.com/posts/nothing-works-and-everyone-is-euphoric/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>If coding has been solved, why does software keep getting worse?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>如果编码已被解决，软件为何还在变坏？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49033004' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 815</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49033004' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 617</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49033004'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Argues that while fundamentals of programming haven&#x27;t been solved, software quality keeps deteriorating due to incentives, complexity, and rushed delivery. The piece critiques hype and explores how teams manage complexity, proposing signals for healthier practices like modularity and testing.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章认为编程基础并未真正被解决，但软件质量仍在恶化，原因在于激励、复杂度与匆促交付。批评行业热潮背后的错配，讨论如何通过模块化、测试等方式提升软件质量。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://ptrchm.com/images/photo.jpg' data-full='https://ptrchm.com/images/photo.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48808482' data-tags='Hardware,Open Source,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48808482' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(51) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://openwrt.org/toh/openwrt/one' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>OpenWrt One – Open Hardware Router</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>OpenWrt One：开源硬件路由器</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48808482' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 812</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48808482' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 312</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48808482'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>OpenWrt One introduces a complete open-hardware router design paired with OpenWrt software, letting users inspect schematics and customize firmware end-to-end. The project signals a move toward verifiably open networking gear that prioritizes user sovereignty, security, and long-term repairability. It could reshape how hobbyists source and build their own routers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>OpenWrt One 将开源硬件路由器设计与 OpenWrt 软件结合，允许用户查看原理图并全流程定制固件。该项目预示着向更可验证的开源网络设备转变，强调用户自主性、安全性及长期可维护性。可能改变爱好者自建路由器的选型与实现方式。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://openwrt.org/.within.website/x/cmd/anubis/static/img/pensive.webp?cacheBuster=1.22.0-20250926' data-full='https://openwrt.org/.within.website/x/cmd/anubis/static/img/pensive.webp?cacheBuster=1.22.0-20250926' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
