<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48792383' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 804</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48792383' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 333</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48792383'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A solo developer&#x27;s train simulator has earned near-universal praise, illustrating how focused scope and polish can rival big studio productions. It underscores indie grit and the potential of passion-driven, high-quality simulations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一名独立开发者打造的铁路模拟游戏获得广泛赞誉，显示出专注与打磨同样能与大厂作品竞争，凸显独立开发者的韧性与高质量仿真潜力。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://kotaku.com/app/uploads/2026/05/06-running-train-1200x675.jpg' data-full='https://kotaku.com/app/uploads/2026/05/06-running-train-1200x675.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48816883' data-tags='Open Source,Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48816883' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(54) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://streetcomplete.app/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>StreetComplete: Fixing OpenStreetMap, one tiny quest at a time</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>StreetComplete：通过一个个小任务修复 OpenStreetMap</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48816883' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 800</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48816883' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 204</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48816883'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>StreetComplete lowers the bar for contributing to OpenStreetMap by asking tiny, focused tasks. The approach increases participation and data completeness without heavy commitment, illustrating a scalable model for crowd-sourced mapping.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>StreetComplete 通过把地图修正变成一个个小任务来激励贡献者，逐步完善 OpenStreetMap 的数据。该模式提高参与度与数据质量，降低参与门槛。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://streetcomplete.app/res/favicon.svg' data-full='https://streetcomplete.app/res/favicon.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49112744' data-tags='Security,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49112744' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(55) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://krebsonsecurity.com/2026/07/read-this-before-you-buy-that-tv-streaming-stick/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Read this before you buy that TV streaming stick</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>买电视流媒体棒前该知道的事</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49112744' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 799</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49112744' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 537</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49112744'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Smart TV sticks often ship with limited security and privacy protections, making them an easy attack vector for malware and data leakage. When shopping, prioritize reputable brands, timely firmware updates, and configurable privacy settings to minimize risk.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>电视流媒体棒常常安全与隐私保护不足，容易成为恶意软件与数据泄露的入口。选购时应关注品牌信誉、固件更新及时性以及可配置的隐私设置，以降低风险。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://krebsonsecurity.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/h96-amazon.png' data-full='https://krebsonsecurity.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/h96-amazon.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48936426' data-tags='Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48936426' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(56) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://opensource.microsoft.com/blog/2026/07/16/microsoft-comic-chat-is-now-open-source/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Microsoft Comic Chat is now open source</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>微软 Comic Chat 现已开源</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48936426' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 798</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48936426' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 173</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48936426'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Microsoft released the classic Comic Chat client as open source, reviving a piece of 1990s online chat UI and protocol. The release provides a sandbox for experimentation with vintage software, preserving artifacts and teaching UX evolution.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>微软将经典的 Comic Chat 客户端开源，重现了 1990 年代的在线聊天界面和协议。此次发布为复古软件的实验、保存与研究提供了机会，也揭示了聊天用户体验的演变。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opensource.microsoft.com/blog/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/Microsoft365-FY26-Consumer-C2-ArtStudio-2749-RGB-scaled.jpg' data-full='https://opensource.microsoft.com/blog/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/Microsoft365-FY26-Consumer-C2-ArtStudio-2749-RGB-scaled.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48930454' data-tags='Culture,Entertainment'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48930454' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(57) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.pigeonsandplanes.com/read/music-piracy-what-cd-oink-nine-inch-nails-streaming' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The lost joy of music piracy</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>音乐盗版的逝去乐趣</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48930454' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 796</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48930454' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 563</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Entertainment'>Entertainment</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48930454'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Streaming turned music into a predictable, licensed product, draining the anarchic joy of early piracy. The piece reflects on how the pirate-era culture created camaraderie and discovery, and asks what we lose when access becomes commodified.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>流媒体让音乐变成可控的授权商品，削弱了早期盗版带来的狂热和社区感。本文回顾那段盗版文化如何塑造了发现与分享的乐趣，并讨论当下更易获取的市场会让我们错过哪些宝贵的文化体验。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.sanity.io/images/pu3h3rl5/production/61dfa8f019d1a62c96f4a72bfb734ede527e0c90-1920x1080.gif?rect=0,39,1920,1003&amp;w=1200&amp;h=627&amp;fit=crop&amp;auto=format' data-full='https://cdn.sanity.io/images/pu3h3rl5/production/61dfa8f019d1a62c96f4a72bfb734ede527e0c90-1920x1080.gif?rect=0,39,1920,1003&amp;w=1200&amp;h=627&amp;fit=crop&amp;auto=format' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49080664' data-tags='Science,DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49080664' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(58) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.data.jma.go.jp/multi/quake/quake_detail.html?eventID=20260728163528&amp;lang=en' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>7.1 Earthquake in Japan</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>日本发生7.1级地震</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49080664' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 795</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49080664' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 219</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49080664'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An about 7.1-magnitude earthquake struck Japan, triggering emergency alerts and shaking across affected regions. Authorities are assessing damage, power outages, and aftershock risks, highlighting resilience needs for infrastructure and critical facilities in disaster response planning.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>日本发生7.1级地震，政府发出紧急警报，震感覆盖多个区域。官方正在评估损毁、断电和余震风险，基础设施和关键设施的韧性成为灾后评估的核心议题。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.data.jma.go.jp/multi/images/common/logo.gif' data-full='https://www.data.jma.go.jp/multi/images/common/logo.gif' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48869989' data-tags='Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48869989' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(59) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://danq.me/2026/07/09/your-app-could-have-been-a-webpage/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Your &#x27;app&#x27; could have been a webpage (so I fixed it for you)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>你的“应用”其实可以是网页版本——我把它改成网页了</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48869989' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 795</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48869989' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 474</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48869989'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A practical take on web vs native apps: many projects can be delivered as a robust webpage, reducing maintenance and improving accessibility. The post shows concrete steps to flatten typical app functionality into a responsive web UI.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>谈及网页与原生应用的现实取舍：很多项目其实可以用强大的网页版本来实现，降低维护成本并提升可访问性。文中给出将常见应用功能转换成响应式网页界面的具体做法。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://danq.me/_q23-og/29641/0.png' data-full='https://danq.me/_q23-og/29641/0.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48783117' data-tags='Health,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48783117' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(60) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.mikebowler.ca/2026/07/03/co2-and-decision-making/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The bottleneck might be the air in the room</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>瓶颈可能在房间里的空气</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48783117' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 789</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48783117' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 455</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48783117'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>CO2 buildup in poorly ventilated spaces can impair decision-making and cognitive performance, setting a hidden bottleneck for teams. The post argues that improving ventilation and air quality is a cost-effective way to boost focus and judgment in meetings and workplaces.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在密闭空间里二氧化碳积聚会削弱决策能力和认知表现，成为团队的潜在瓶颈。文章强调通过改善通风和空气质量是提升专注力与判断力的成本效益办法，尤其在会议和工作场所。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://files.gargoylesoftware.com/images/mike_bowler.jpg' data-full='https://files.gargoylesoftware.com/images/mike_bowler.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49098965' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(61) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.superlogical.com/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Superlogical</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>超逻辑</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49098965' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 776</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49098965' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 452</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49098965'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A new project named Superlogical surfaces, signaling a push into advanced tooling around logical reasoning and automation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个名为 Superlogical 的新项目浮出水面，标志着在逻辑推理与自动化工具领域的推进。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.superlogical.com/og.webp' data-full='https://www.superlogical.com/og.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48841676' data-tags='Programming,Open Source,Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48841676' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(62) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/malisper/pgrust' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Postgres rewritten in Rust, now passing 100% of the Postgres regression tests</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>用 Rust 重写 PostgreSQL，现已通过 100% 回归测试</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48841676' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 776</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48841676' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 678</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48841676'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A project to rewrite PostgreSQL components in Rust reports passing all regression tests, signaling Rust&#x27;s viability for safety-critical system software. While not a drop-in replacement, the effort demonstrates how safer memory management and modern tooling could influence database internals.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>用 Rust 重写 PostgreSQL 组件的尝试声称已通过全部回归测试，显示 Rust 在安全性关键系统中的潜力。虽然不是直接的替代，但这项工作展示了更安全的内存管理与现代工具链对数据库内部实现的潜在影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/a59ef3a90431cd3a74201544f1dcdb2c538a81e916a327bfd0c589ffc9b5582f/malisper/pgrust' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/a59ef3a90431cd3a74201544f1dcdb2c538a81e916a327bfd0c589ffc9b5582f/malisper/pgrust' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48808928' data-tags='Open Source,Data,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48808928' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(63) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.comaps.app/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>CoMaps – FOSS Offline Maps</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>CoMaps：开源离线地图</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48808928' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 776</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48808928' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 205</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48808928'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>CoMaps is an open-source offline mapping project that lets you navigate without internet access. It emphasizes privacy and local control by bundling downloadable map data and routing logic, avoiding reliance on centralized services. This makes it appealing for privacy-minded users and developers who want a portable, maintainable map solution.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>CoMaps 是一个开源离线地图项目，支持在没有互联网时进行导航。它强调隐私与本地控制，提供可下载的地图数据与路由逻辑，避免对集中服务的依赖。对注重隐私的用户和开发者而言，这是一种可移植、可维护的地图方案。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.comaps.app/images/sections/map-s-3.webp' data-full='https://www.comaps.app/images/sections/map-s-3.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48823557' data-tags='Privacy,Politics,Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48823557' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(64) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://allaboutcookies.org/eu-mandatory-distracted-driver-system' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Every new car sold in the European Union must include a driver monitoring camera</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>欧盟规定所有新车必须装配驾驶员监控摄像头</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48823557' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 773</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48823557' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1043</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48823557'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Europe&#x27;s new rule mandates driver monitoring cameras in all new cars, aiming to curb distracted driving and fatigue-related accidents. The requirement will push automakers to integrate interior cameras and analytics, raising privacy, data handling, and compliance concerns. Given the EU market&#x27;s scale, this could set a de facto global safety standard for vehicle interiors.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>欧盟的新规要求所有新车配备驾驶员监控摄像头，旨在减少疲劳驾驶和分心驾驶导致的事故。厂商需在车内安装摄像头并接入分析功能，同时引发隐私保护、数据处理与合规性方面的担忧。凭借其市场规模，该举或将为全球车辆内饰安全标准定调。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48973869' data-tags='Science,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48973869' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(65) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://xcancel.com/__alpoge__/status/2079028340955197566' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Claude Fable produced a counterexample to the Jacobian Conjecture</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Claude Fable 提出雅可比猜想的反例</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48973869' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 772</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48973869' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 482</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48973869'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A claimed counterexample to the Jacobian Conjecture circulated, challenging a long-standing open problem in algebraic geometry. Such claims are subjected to rigorous verification, as a verified counterexample would upend established theory.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>据称出现了针对雅可比猜想的反例，令这一长期悬而未决的问题成为焦点。此类主张需要严格验证，一旦成立将彻底改变相关理论。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49068738' data-tags='AI,Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49068738' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(66) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://twitter.com/HedgieMarkets/status/2081534588485296565' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AI companies are shredding rare books</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI公司正在撕碎珍稀书籍</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49068738' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 771</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49068738' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 491</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49068738'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Reports claim AI companies are shredding rare books to feed training data, raising alarms about data sourcing and cultural preservation. The trend highlights the tension between rapid AI development and safeguarding irreplaceable artifacts, prompting calls for governance and licensing reforms. Experts warn the losses could be irreversible if these materials are discarded before proper archival decisions are made.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>有报道称AI公司在收集训练数据时撕碎珍稀书籍，这引发对数据来源与文化保护的担忧。此趋势突显在推动AI高速发展时保护不可替代文献的难题，促使推动治理与许可框架的改革。若属实，损失可能是不可逆的，相关机构被敦促做出更审慎的档案保存决策。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://pbs.twimg.com/media/HOMX3t1XYAA3Pr7.jpg' data-full='https://pbs.twimg.com/media/HOMX3t1XYAA3Pr7.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49112232' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(67) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.blog/changelog/2026-07-30-stacked-pull-requests-are-now-in-public-preview/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Stacked PRs are now live on GitHub</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>GitHub 现已上线堆叠 PR（Stacked PRs）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49112232' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 767</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49112232' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 290</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49112232'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Stacked PRs enable multiple dependent pull requests to be submitted and reviewed together, currently in public preview. This workflow can streamline complex changes, reduce review churn, and improve collaboration.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>堆叠 PR 允许将相互依赖的多个合并请求打包提交与评审，现处于公开预览阶段。该工作流有助于简化复杂改动、降低评审成本、提升协作效率。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://github.blog/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/Changelog-Stacks-NewRelease-Unfurl.png' data-full='https://github.blog/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/Changelog-Stacks-NewRelease-Unfurl.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Grok 4.5 extends the AI assistant&#x27;s capabilities with better memory, smarter task planning, and improved tool integration. The release emphasizes more reliable automation and streamlined workflows for building AI-powered assistants.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Grok 4.5 在记忆、任务规划及对外部工具接入方面有显著提升，提升了自动化的可靠性。此次更新聚焦为构建 AI 助手提供更顺畅的工作流和更高的可用性。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(69) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://bun.com/blog/bun-in-rust' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Rewriting Bun in Rust</a></p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48837877'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Bun, the fast JavaScript runtime, is being rewritten in Rust, a move aimed at boosting performance and ecosystem stability, but it also raises questions about compatibility.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Bun 的核心将用 Rust 重写，旨在提升性能与生态稳定性，但也带来兼容性与迁移方面的挑战。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49118781' data-tags='Security,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49118781' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(70) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://earendil.com/posts/session-portability/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The session you cannot take with you</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>你带不走的会话</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49118781' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 751</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49118781' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 213</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49118781'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Session portability is a growing problem as users expect to pick up where they left off across devices. The piece surveys approaches like portable session tokens, device linking, and cryptographic handoffs, weighing security and UX trade-offs. The takeaway is that truly portable sessions could unlock seamless multi-device experiences.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>随着用户期望在多设备间无缝继续工作，会话可移植性成为一个日益突出的问题。文中探讨了可移植会话令牌、设备绑定与密钥交接等方案，并权衡安全性与用户体验的取舍。真正的会话可移植性或将解锁无缝的多设备使用体验。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://earendil.com/static/og/posts/session-portability.png' data-full='https://earendil.com/static/og/posts/session-portability.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Transcribe.cpp 实现</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48963879' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 750</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48963879' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 158</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48963879'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Transcribe.cpp presents a compact C++ project that demonstrates a speech-to-text workflow. It walks through building a lightweight transcription pipeline and discusses latency versus accuracy trade-offs, including how to structure streaming versus batch transcription.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Transcribe.cpp 展示了一个精简的 C++ 转写实现，展示了将音频转换为文本的基本流程，并讨论了延迟与准确率之间的权衡，包含流式与批量转写的取舍。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48993414' data-tags='AI'>

<p class='hn-title'>(72) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.google/innovation-and-ai/models-and-research/gemini-models/gemini-3-6-flash-3-5-flash-lite-3-5-flash-cyber/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Gemini 3.6 Flash, 3.5 Flash-Lite, and 3.5 Flash Cyber</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Gemini 3.6 Flash、3.5 Flash-Lite 与 3.5 Flash-Cyber</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48993414' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 746</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48993414' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 572</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48993414'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Google rolls out Gemini 3.6 alongside two lighter/specialized variants: 3.5 Flash-Lite and 3.5 Flash-Cyber. The Flash family targets faster, lighter inference for consumer apps and code-related tasks, expanding Gemini&#x27;s reach across devices and workloads. This tiered lineup signals Google&#x27;s continued push to optimize latency, cost, and safety across use cases.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>谷歌推出 Gemini 3.6，并辅以两款轻量化/专业化的 Flash 版本：3.5 Flash-Lite 与 3.5 Flash-Cyber。Flash 系列强调在消费级应用和代码场景下的低延迟、低资源占用，拓展 Gemini 的适用场景。此举体现谷歌在用例分层、降低成本与提升安全性方面的持续布局。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://storage.googleapis.com/gweb-uniblog-publish-prod/images/gemini-3-5_3-6_3-5-Cyber__key-art__statement_.width-1300.jpg' data-full='https://storage.googleapis.com/gweb-uniblog-publish-prod/images/gemini-3-5_3-6_3-5-Cyber__key-art__statement_.width-1300.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48834405' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(73) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://openai.com/index/introducing-gpt-live/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>GPT‑Live</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>GPT-Live</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48834405' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 745</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48834405' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 520</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48834405'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>GPT-Live introduces live, real-time interactions with updated data and external tools, enabling more dynamic conversations. It signals a shift toward apps that continuously consume streams of information rather than static prompts.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>GPT-Live 引入实时、动态的数据和外部工具交互，使对话具有更即时的更新能力。此举推动应用从静态提示向持续获取信息的对话体验转变。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49032660' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(74) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://glyphack.com/attention/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>It&#x27;s getting harder to focus every day</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>专注力每天都在下降</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49032660' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 744</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49032660' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 402</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49032660'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Focus is slipping in an age of constant distractions—from notifications to algorithmic feeds. The piece suggests practical shifts like batching deep work, reducing context switching, and hardening personal workflows to reclaim attention.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在持续的通知和算法信息流冲击下，专注力日益下降。文中给出减少分心、将深度工作集中在固定时段，以及优化日常工作流等应对策略。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48843352' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48843352' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(75) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://andrewkelley.me/post/my-thoughts-bun-rust-rewrite.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>My thoughts on the Bun Rust rewrite</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我对 Bun 的 Rust 重写的一些看法</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48843352' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 738</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48843352' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 641</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48843352'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Bun project is exploring a Rust rewrite to address safety and performance limitations of the original runtime. The note discusses why a Rust-based core could improve reliability and tooling integration, while also outlining migration and compatibility challenges that come with rewriting a mature platform.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>该文章探讨 Bun 以 Rust 重写核心的原因，强调安全性和性能提升的潜力，以及对可靠性与工具链集成的影响。还提及迁移路线和兼容性挑战等在对成熟平台进行重写时需要权衡的问题。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48861717' data-tags='Security,Privacy,Legal'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48861717' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(76) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.jeffgeerling.com/blog/2026/quadrf-can-spot-drones-and-see-wifi-through-my-wall/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>QuadRF can spot drones and see WiFi through my wall</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>QuadRF 能侦测无人机并穿墙探测 WiFi 信号</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48861717' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 736</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48861717' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 231</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48861717'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>QuadRF demonstrates that RF sensing can reveal both drone activity and WiFi signals through walls. This kind of passive observation highlights security and privacy implications for home networks and aerial devices, even when devices aren&#x27;t in visible sight. It also underscores practical limits like range, clutter, and legal boundaries.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>QuadRF 展示了射频探测可以在墙后揭示无人机活动和 WiFi 信号。这类被动观测引发对家庭网络和无人机安全与隐私的担忧，同时也暴露出覆盖范围、信号干扰和法律边界等局限。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.jeffgeerling.com/blog/2026/quadrf-can-spot-drones-and-see-wifi-through-my-wall/quadrf-antennna-array.jpeg' data-full='https://www.jeffgeerling.com/blog/2026/quadrf-can-spot-drones-and-see-wifi-through-my-wall/quadrf-antennna-array.jpeg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48745456' data-tags='Gaming,Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48745456' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(77) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.playstation.com/2026/07/01/physical-disc-production-ending-in-january-2028-for-new-games-releasing-on-playstation-consoles/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Physical disc production ending in Jan 2028 for new games on PlayStation</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>PlayStation 将于 2028 年 1 月停止新作实体光盘生产</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48745456' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 733</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48745456' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 742</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48745456'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Sony will end physical disc production for new PlayStation releases in January 2028, accelerating its digital-first strategy. The move reduces manufacturing and distribution costs but may affect collectors, retailers, and regions with stronger physical markets. It signals a lasting shift in how console games are distributed and consumed.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>索尼将于 2028 年 1 月停止新作实体光盘的生产，进一步推动数字化优先策略。此举降低了制造与发行成本，但可能影响收藏者、零售商以及实体市场较强的地区，预示着主机游戏发行与消费方式的长期转变。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://blog.playstation.com/tachyon/2026/07/70bb22030b29f4234fd812253bf1129b00609c4c.jpg' data-full='https://blog.playstation.com/tachyon/2026/07/70bb22030b29f4234fd812253bf1129b00609c4c.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48901569' data-tags='Energy,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48901569' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(78) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://tech.supercarblondie.com/japan-recovers-up-to-90-of-lithium-from-used-ev-batteries/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Japan develops a method to recover up to 90% of lithium from used EV batteries</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>日本开发可从废旧新能源汽车电池回收高达90%的锂</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48901569' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 733</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48901569' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 190</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48901569'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Japan has demonstrated a process capable of recovering up to 90% of lithium from spent EV batteries, boosting recycling efficiency and extending battery supply resilience. The advance could lower material costs and lessen environmental impact by reducing mining and refining needs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>日本展示了一种可从废旧电动车电池中回收锂高达90%的工艺，显著提升回收效率并加强电池材料保障。此举可能降低成本、减轻采矿与精炼带来的环境压力。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://tech.supercarblondie.com/cdn-cgi/image/width=1200,height=675,quality=80,format=webp,fit=cover/wp-content/uploads/Japan-lithium-featured.webp' data-full='https://tech.supercarblondie.com/cdn-cgi/image/width=1200,height=675,quality=80,format=webp,fit=cover/wp-content/uploads/Japan-lithium-featured.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48804993' data-tags='Gaming,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48804993' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(79) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://news.xbox.com/en-us/2026/07/06/resetting-xbox/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Resetting Xbox</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Xbox 重置</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48804993' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 730</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48804993' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 912</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48804993'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Microsoft outlines reset options for Xbox consoles, detailing when to perform a factory reset versus a simpler reboot and what data stays or is erased. The guidance aims to give players a reliable recovery path for performance issues, software glitches, or corrupted saves. The update underscores the importance of predictable recovery flows as consoles grow more complex.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>微软为 Xbox 主机列出重置选项，说明何时执行出厂重置、何时进行简单重启，以及数据保留或清除的范围。该指南为应对性能问题、软件故障或数据损坏提供可靠的恢复路径。随着主机软件日益复杂，可靠的恢复流程显得尤为重要。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://xboxwire.thesourcemediaassets.com/sites/2/2026/05/Bootup_Wire-9c068aa206c9a72d2b1f.png' data-full='https://xboxwire.thesourcemediaassets.com/sites/2/2026/05/Bootup_Wire-9c068aa206c9a72d2b1f.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(80) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://clashreport.com/world/articles/spain-orders-blacklist-of-us-tech-giant-palantir-from-public-and-private-companies-fsnc2z17gjv' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Spain Orders Blacklist of Palantir from Public and Private Companies</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>西班牙将 Palantir 列入公私部门黑名单</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48762725' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 729</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48762725' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 301</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49095865' data-tags='Open Source,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49095865' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(81) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://koreader.rocks/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>KOReader</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>KOReader：跨平台的开源电子书阅读器</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49095865' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 720</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49095865' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 232</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49095865'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>KOReader remains a robust, open-source e-reader that supports a wide range of formats and platforms, including e-ink devices. It emphasizes customization, annotations, and privacy-friendly features not always available in closed apps.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>KOReader 依然是一款强大的跨平台开源电子书阅读器，支持多种格式与设备，包括电子墨水设备。它强调可定制、注释功能以及在封闭应用中不易实现的隐私友好特性。文章凸显了KOReader如何继续赋能读者，让用户对设备拥有更多控制权。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://koreader.rocks/koreader-logo.png' data-full='https://koreader.rocks/koreader-logo.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48746445' data-tags='Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48746445' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(82) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://wangcong.org/2026-06-30-why-i-stopped-arguing-with-people.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Most arguments are about ego, not ideas</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>大多數爭論源於自尊心，而非觀點</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48746445' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 712</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48746445' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 556</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48746445'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Most online disagreements stem from ego rather than substantive ideas. The piece argues for stepping away from heated exchanges and focusing on listening and shared goals, which can make discussions more productive and informative for tech communities.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>大多數在線爭論源於自尊心，而非實質性觀點。文章倡導在情緒升溫時停止爭辯，專注傾聽並明確共同目標，讓技術社群的討論更高效、具啟發性。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48919363' data-tags='Health,Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48919363' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(83) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://academic.oup.com/sleep/article/47/1/zsad253/7280269' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Sleep regularity is a stronger predictor of mortality risk than sleep duration (2023)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>睡眠规律性比睡眠时长更能预测死亡风险</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48919363' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 712</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48919363' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 384</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48919363'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>New research suggests that how regularly you sleep predicts mortality risk more strongly than total sleep duration. Using long-term wearable data, the study links consistent sleep timing with better health outcomes, independent of hours slept. The finding shifts emphasis toward regular schedules as a health strategy and dovetails with consumer wellness tech.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>最新研究表明，睡眠时间的规律性与死亡风险的相关性比总睡眠时长更强。通过长期可穿戴设备数据，研究发现稳定的睡眠时序与更好的健康结果相关，且与实际睡眠时长无关。该发现将健康策略的重点从睡眠时长转向规律作息，并与可穿戴健康科技的发展相吻合。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49119559' data-tags='Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49119559' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(84) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://api-docs.deepseek.com/updates/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>DeepSeek-V4-Flash Update</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>DeepSeek-V4-Flash 更新</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49119559' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 708</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49119559' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 335</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49119559'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>DeepSeek releases a firmware update for the V4 Flash platform, focusing on stability, performance, and new features for data indexing and retrieval. The update underscores ongoing optimization of flash-based search systems and developer tooling.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>DeepSeek 为 V4 Flash 平台推出固件更新，聚焦稳定性、性能，并带来对数据索引与检索的新功能。此次更新凸显对基于闪存的搜索系统及开发工具的持续优化。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://api-docs.deepseek.com/img/deepseek-social-card.jpeg' data-full='https://api-docs.deepseek.com/img/deepseek-social-card.jpeg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48833715' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48833715' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(85) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://devblogs.microsoft.com/typescript/announcing-typescript-7-0/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>TypeScript 7</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>TypeScript 7</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48833715' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 707</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48833715' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 298</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48833715'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>TypeScript 7 brings major improvements to the type system, smarter inference, and faster builds, plus new compiler features that reduce runtime errors. It also updates project configuration and editor tooling.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>TypeScript 7 对类型系统、推断能力和构建速度带来重大改进，并引入新的编译器特性以降低运行时错误。此次发布还对项目配置和编辑器工具链进行了更新。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://devblogs.microsoft.com/typescript/wp-content/uploads/sites/11/2026/07/ts7-0-stable-1-1.png' data-full='https://devblogs.microsoft.com/typescript/wp-content/uploads/sites/11/2026/07/ts7-0-stable-1-1.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48936584' data-tags='Design,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48936584' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(86) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.mixfont.com/experiments/decoy-font' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Decoy Font</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Decoy 字体</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48936584' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 705</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48936584' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 157</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48936584'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Decoy Font is an experimental typeface designed to confuse automated readers and OCR systems by mapping glyphs in unusual ways. It highlights typography’s impact on AI text detectors and reveals current recognition limits.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Decoy 字体是一种实验性字体，通过以异常的方式映射字形来混淆自动识别系统与 OCR。它揭示排版对 AI 文本检测的影响与当前识别能力的边界。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://static.mixfont.com/assets/20260715-083635-decoy-font-og-ctfx685m.webp' data-full='https://static.mixfont.com/assets/20260715-083635-decoy-font-og-ctfx685m.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48978605' data-tags='Security,Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48978605' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(87) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://news.risky.biz/risky-bulletin-hacker-wipes-romanias-entire-land-registry-database/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Hacker wipes Romania&#x27;s land registry database</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>黑客抹除罗马尼亚土地登记数据库</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48978605' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 703</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48978605' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 402</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48978605'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A hacker reportedly wiped Romania&#x27;s entire land registry database, threatening the integrity of property records and disrupting transactions. The incident highlights catastrophic risks from insufficient backups, disaster recovery planning, and the high stakes of protecting centralized public registries.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>有黑客据称清除了罗马尼亚整个土地登记数据库，威胁到财产记录的完整性并干扰交易。此事凸显了集中式公共登记在备份、灾难恢复和安全保护不足时会带来的严重风险。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://storage.ghost.io/c/16/e9/16e9a748-ca66-4b3c-8590-85537131f696/content/images/2026/07/000-RBN-logo-4.png' data-full='https://storage.ghost.io/c/16/e9/16e9a748-ca66-4b3c-8590-85537131f696/content/images/2026/07/000-RBN-logo-4.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48996318' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(88) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://freeink.org/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>FreeInk: Open ecosystem for e-readers</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Freeink：开源电子书阅读器开放生态</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48996318' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 703</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48996318' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 157</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48996318'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Freeink aims to create an open ecosystem for e-readers, enabling interoperability among devices, apps, and content. By reducing vendor lock-in, it could accelerate community-driven improvements and privacy options.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Freeink 旨在建立面向电子书阅读器的开放生态，促进设备、应用与内容之间的互操作性。降低厂商锁定可能推动社区驱动的改进，并提升隐私选项。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://freeink.org/og-image.png' data-full='https://freeink.org/og-image.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49084938' data-tags='Privacy,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49084938' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(89) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://citizens-initiative.europa.eu/initiatives/details/2026/000011_en' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Stop Killing the Internet: No Digital ID and No Age Verification</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>停止肆意‘杀死互联网’：拒绝数字身份与年龄验证</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49084938' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 701</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49084938' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 283</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49084938'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A campaign arguing against digital IDs and age verification for online services, advocating privacy-preserving options.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一项倡议反对在线服务中的数字身份和年龄验证，主张隐私友好、可替代的保护方案。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://citizens-initiative.europa.eu/profiles/contrib/ewcms/modules/ewcms_seo/assets/images/eu-socialmedia-fallback.png' data-full='https://citizens-initiative.europa.eu/profiles/contrib/ewcms/modules/ewcms_seo/assets/images/eu-socialmedia-fallback.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48883275' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48883275' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(90) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://systima.ai/blog/claude-code-vs-opencode-token-overhead' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Claude Code sends 33k tokens before reading the prompt; OpenCode sends 7k</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Claude Code 在读取提示前消耗 3.3 万令牌；OpenCode 仅约 7 千令牌</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48883275' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 689</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48883275' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 380</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48883275'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Claude Code consumes about 3.3万 tokens before the user prompt is parsed, while OpenCode uses roughly 7,000 tokens. The gap highlights how different toolchains incur context overhead that affects latency, cost, and developer workflows in AI-assisted coding. This emphasizes the importance of managing token budgets when chaining multiple AI services.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Claude Code 在读取用户提示前就消耗约 3.3 万令牌，而 OpenCode 约 7 千令牌。这一差距揭示了不同工具链在上下文开销上的差异，进而影响 AI 辅助编码的延迟、成本和工作流。强调在串联多家 AI 服务时对令牌预算进行管理的重要性。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48910545' data-tags='AI,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48910545' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(91) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://prismml.com/news/bonsai-27b' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Bonsai 27B: A 27B-Class model that runs on a phone</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Bonsai 27B：能在手机上运行的27B级模型</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48910545' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 687</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48910545' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 243</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48910545'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Bonsai 27B project demonstrates a 27-billion-parameter model that can run directly on a smartphone, signaling a practical edge-AI milestone. Achieving on-device inference requires aggressive optimization (pruning, quantization, efficient runtimes) to fit memory and power constraints, but it promises lower latency and stronger privacy. This shift hints at a future where powerful AI can operate without constant cloud access.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Bonsai 27B 展示了一个27B参数级模型能够直接在手机上运行，标志着边缘AI的一个实际里程碑。实现本地推理需要对模型进行大幅优化（裁剪、量化、高效运行时），以适应内存与电池限制，但可带来更低的延迟和更强的隐私保护。这预示着强大AI有望在不依赖云端的情况下运行。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/697a3312d33c2cc715ec3899/69961847735f3ec32aafc18e_prism-logo.svg' data-full='https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/697a3312d33c2cc715ec3899/69961847735f3ec32aafc18e_prism-logo.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48997221' data-tags='Privacy,Legal'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48997221' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(92) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.techradar.com/vpn/vpn-privacy-security/vpns-are-lawful-technical-tools-says-eu-court-in-landmark-anne-frank-copyright-ruling' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#x27;VPNs are lawful technical tools,&#x27; says EU Court in landmark copyright ruling</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>欧盟法院裁定 VPN 为合法技术工具</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48997221' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 685</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48997221' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 139</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48997221'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The EU Court ruled that VPNs are lawful technical tools with legitimate use cases in protecting privacy and enabling access to information. This ruling clarifies that circumvention or privacy protections are not inherently illegal, shaping how copyright enforcement and online freedom interplay. It signals a supportive stance for privacy-preserving tech in Europe.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>欧盟法院裁定 VPN 为合法的技术工具，强调其在保护隐私和访问信息方面的正当用途。该判决澄清了 VPN 使用本身并非非法，对提供商与用户均有积极影响，同时为欧洲的版权执法与互联网自由定调。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.mos.cms.futurecdn.net/5NsvMB6arEjzGkWxkstoDj-1920-80.jpg' data-full='https://cdn.mos.cms.futurecdn.net/5NsvMB6arEjzGkWxkstoDj-1920-80.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(93) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://maurycyz.com/projects/bad_jpeg/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Regressive JPEGs</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>回归式 JPEG 图像</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48954851' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 683</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48954851' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 68</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A provocative look at JPEG compression artifacts and how regressive techniques alter image quality.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇文章探讨 JPEG 压缩中的伪影，以及回归式方法如何改变或揭示这种压缩行为。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49020999' data-tags='Business,Finance,AI,Health'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49020999' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(94) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://futurism.com/artificial-intelligence/ai-companies-hide-debt-off-balance-sheet' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AI Companies Are Trying to Hide a Staggering Amount of Debt</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 公司正隐藏巨额债务</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49020999' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 683</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49020999' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 372</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49020999'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>AI firms have reportedly buried large debt off their balance sheets, highlighting a riskier funding landscape as capital markets back AI bets. The disclosure questions the true financial health of high-flying AI startups and their burn rates.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>据称 AI 公司在账面之外隐藏了巨额债务，暴露在对 AI 投资热潮中的资金风险。此类披露质疑这些高增长企业的真实财务状况和资金消耗速度。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://futurism.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/ai-companies-hide-debt-off-balance-sheet.jpg?w=1200' data-full='https://futurism.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/ai-companies-hide-debt-off-balance-sheet.jpg?w=1200' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48786781' data-tags='Privacy,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48786781' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(95) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://javoriuski.com/post/youtube' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Leaking YouTube creators&#x27; private videos</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>泄露 YouTube 创作者的私人视频</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48786781' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 681</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48786781' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 392</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48786781'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Private videos of YouTube creators have surfaced, underscoring how gaps in privacy controls and access management can leak sensitive content. The incident spotlights the friction between creator workflows, platform safeguards, and third-party storage or collaboration tools. It reinforces the need for strict access auditing, multi-factor authentication, and encrypted pipelines in large creator ecosystems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>有些 YouTube 创作者的私人视频被泄露，暴露出隐私控制与访问管理的薄弱环节。事件凸显了创作者工作流、平台防护与第三方存储/协作工具之间的矛盾。需要加强访问审计、启用多因素认证，以及在大型创作者生态系统中实施端到端加密和更严格的数据管控。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://javoriuski.com/assets/youtube.jpg' data-full='https://javoriuski.com/assets/youtube.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48794750' data-tags='Gaming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48794750' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(96) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://popcar.bearblog.dev/its-about-ownership/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>It&#x27;s not about physical vs. digital games, it&#x27;s about ownership</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>不是实体/数字游戏之争，而是关于所有权的问题</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48794750' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 677</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48794750' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 515</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48794750'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues that ownership—not format—drives value in gaming, covering DRM, portability, and platform control that shape how players access and keep games.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章指出，游戏真正的分水岭在于所有权，而非物理还是数字的形式；包括数字版权管理、跨平台可移植性和平台控制等因素，决定玩家对游戏的长期拥有与访问。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://popcar.bearblog.dev/static/og-image.png' data-full='https://popcar.bearblog.dev/static/og-image.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48809877' data-tags='AI,Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48809877' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(97) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://martinalderson.com/posts/the-upcoming-ai-margin-collapse-part-1-glm-5-2/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>GLM 5.2 and the coming AI margin collapse</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>GLM 5.2 与 AI 利润率的收缩来临</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48809877' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 676</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48809877' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 462</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48809877'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>GLM 5.2 illustrates continued improvements in language models, but the piece argues that margins for AI services may compress as compute costs rise and competition intensifies. It examines implications for developers, vendors, and the economics of AI deployments.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>GLM 5.2 展示了语言模型的持续进步，但文章认为随着计算成本上升与竞争加剧，AI 服务的利润率可能收缩。讨论了对开发者、厂商和 AI 部署经济性的影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://martinalderson.com/img/og/glm-5-2-and-the-coming-ai-margin-collapse-part-1.png' data-full='https://martinalderson.com/img/og/glm-5-2-and-the-coming-ai-margin-collapse-part-1.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48788283' data-tags='Gaming,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48788283' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(98) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/ammaarreshi/Generals-Mac-iOS-iPad/tree/main' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Command and Conquer Generals natively ported to macOS, iPhone, iPad using Fable</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>使用 Fable 将《Command &amp; Conquer: Generals》原生移植到 macOS、iPhone、iPad</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48788283' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 673</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48788283' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 283</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48788283'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Fans have natively ported Command &amp; Conquer Generals to macOS, iPhone, and iPad using Fable. The port demonstrates how modern cross-compile toolchains can revive classic PC games on Apple devices, but licensing and control questions remain.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>粉丝项目将《Command &amp; Conquer Generals》原生移植到 macOS、iPhone 与 iPad，使用 Fable 实现跨平台运行。此举展示了当代跨平台工具链在复活经典 PC 游戏方面的潜力，同时也引发关于授权和设备支持的讨论。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/6feb14451994ca7caac0049e817aeae3b73c0801317c3c71d8f08bcc47b12e7a/ammaarreshi/Generals-Mac-iOS-iPad' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/6feb14451994ca7caac0049e817aeae3b73c0801317c3c71d8f08bcc47b12e7a/ammaarreshi/Generals-Mac-iOS-iPad' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49010129' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49010129' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(99) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://dylancastillo.co/posts/pelicanmaxxing.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Are AI labs pelicanmaxxing?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 研究室到底是在追逐 Pelicanmaxxing 吗？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49010129' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 673</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49010129' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 240</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49010129'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece questions whether AI labs optimize for sensational growth and hype over careful safety and long-term research, coining &#x27;pelicanmaxxing&#x27; as a metaphor. It argues for clearer incentives and more responsible development practices.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章质疑 AI 研究室是否以引人注目的扩张和炒作来取代稳健的安全与长期研究，并以“Pelicanmaxxing”作为隐喻。提出需要更清晰的激励与更负责任的开发方式。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://dylancastillo.co/posts/images/social_media_card.webp' data-full='https://dylancastillo.co/posts/images/social_media_card.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(102) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arstechnica.com/space/2026/07/indias-first-privately-developed-rocket-reaches-orbit-on-dramatic-debut-launch/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>India&#x27;s first privately-developed rocket reaches orbit on debut launch</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>印度首枚私营开发火箭首次进入轨道</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48973835' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 667</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48973835' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 206</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Space'>Space</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48973835'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>India&#x27;s first privately-developed rocket reached orbit on its debut, signaling a new era for the country&#x27;s private space sector. The milestone underscores growing private capabilities, potential disruption to traditional launch dynamics, and global competition in access to space.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>印度首枚私营开发火箭在首秀中进入轨道，标志着印度私营航天进入新阶段。此成就显示私人能力的提升，或对传统发射格局带来影响，并反映全球在进入太空领域的竞争加剧。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(103) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://gamefromscratch.com/microsoft-fire-idtech-team-at-id-software/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Microsoft fire idTech team at Id software</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>微软解雇 Id Software 的 IdTech 团队</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48819244' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 663</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48819244' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 591</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Entertainment'>Entertainment</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48819244'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Microsoft reportedly fired the IdTech team at id Software, signaling a broader reorganization of its game-engine and studio strategy. The move may affect ongoing engine maintenance and future collaboration on titles that rely on Id Tech.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>微软据传解雇了来自 Id Software 的 IdTech 团队，显示出其在游戏引擎和工作室战略上的整合调整。此举可能影响遗留引擎的维护以及依赖 Id Tech 的项目未来合作。</p>
