<p class='hn-title'>(104) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://overpaid.lol' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>OverpAId – Fire your CEO. Hire the future</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>OverpAId：解雇CEO，雇佣未来</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49004663' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 662</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49004663' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 347</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49004663'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A provocative take on AI leadership that envisions firing human CEOs in favor of AI executives. It outlines potential benefits like faster decision cycles and lower costs, but also flags governance, accountability, and oversight risks in AI-driven organizations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个颠覆性观点，设想用 AI 取代人类首席执行官。论述了更快的决策周期和成本节省等潜在好处，同时指出在 AI 驱动的企业中治理、问责和监督等风险。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(105) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.techdirt.com/2026/07/15/sony-deletes-a-bunch-more-movies-from-the-accounts-of-people-who-bought-them/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Sony deletes more movies from the accounts of people who ‘bought’ them</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>索尼再次从购买者账户中删除多部电影</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48933419' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 661</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48933419' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 410</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48933419'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Sony has removed additional titles from users’ digital libraries after purchase, prompting questions about ownership, DRM, and what it means to &#x27;own&#x27; digital media. The incident spotlights ongoing tensions in rights, licensing, and user trust.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>索尼再次从已购买用户的数字库中删除多部电影，引发关于数字所有权、DRM 与所有权概念的讨论。此事凸显版权、许可与用户信任之间的张力。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48981620' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(106) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://jelly-ui.com/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Jelly UI: Soft-body physics for native HTML form controls</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Jelly UI：原生 HTML 表单控件的软体物理</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48981620' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 656</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48981620' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 201</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48981620'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Jelly UI experiments with soft-body physics applied to native HTML form controls, delivering more tactile interactions without heavy dependencies. If adopted, it could influence micro-interactions across web forms and spark new design ideas.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Jelly UI 将软体物理应用到原生 HTML 表单控件，带来更具触感的交互，且无需引入较重的依赖。若普及，可能影响表单的微交互设计并激发新的设计灵感。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49083314' data-tags='Health,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49083314' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(107) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.lji.org/news-events/news/post/new-hiv-vaccine-shows-unprecedented-success-in-preclinical-study/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>New HIV vaccine shows unprecedented success in preclinical study</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>新型HIV疫苗在前临床研究中取得前所未有的成功</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49083314' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 654</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49083314' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 290</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49083314'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A new HIV vaccine candidate shows unprecedented efficacy in preclinical studies, signaling potential breakthrough. However, preclinical success does not guarantee human efficacy; several hurdles remain. If replicated in early human trials, it could reshape HIV vaccine development and open new avenues for immunogen design.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一款新型HIV疫苗候选在前临床研究中展现前所未有的有效性，给HIV疫苗研发带来希望。需要注意的是，前临床结果并不保证在人类身上也有效，仍有多项挑战待解。若在早期人体试验中得到证实，这一突破可能推动疫苗设计的进一步突破，并改变相关研究方向。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49035303' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49035303' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(108) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.cnbc.com/2026/07/24/nvidia-microsoft-meta-open-weight-ai-models.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Nvidia, Microsoft, Meta warn against overregulating open-weight models</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>英伟达、微软、Meta 警告勿过度监管开放权重模型</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49035303' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 650</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49035303' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 313</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49035303'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>NVIDIA, Microsoft, and Meta warn against overregulating open-weight models, arguing that heavy-handed rules could hamper innovation and push developers to closed ecosystems. They advocate targeted, risk-based oversight rather than broad bans, stressing practical governance over blanket prohibitions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>英伟达、微软、Meta 警告不要对开放权重模型进行过度监管，担心会抑制创新并推动开发者转向封闭生态系统。他们主张以风险为基础的、有针对性的监管，而非全面禁令，强调治理应兼顾现实世界的应用场景。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://image.cnbcfm.com/api/v1/image/108173882-1752844539526-gettyimages-2225696381-mms28707_1cm2uml7.jpeg?v=1752844582&amp;w=1920&amp;h=1080' data-full='https://image.cnbcfm.com/api/v1/image/108173882-1752844539526-gettyimages-2225696381-mms28707_1cm2uml7.jpeg?v=1752844582&amp;w=1920&amp;h=1080' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48916975' data-tags='AI,Privacy,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48916975' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(109) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.ayush.digital/blog/the-memory-heist' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I tricked Claude into leaking your deepest, darkest secrets</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我如何骗过 Claude 让它泄露你最深的秘密</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48916975' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 647</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48916975' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 290</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48916975'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A crafted prompt demonstrates a vulnerability in Claude&#x27;s handling of sensitive data, revealing how advanced LLMs can leak information under certain prompts. The post underscores the data-privacy risk and the importance of guardrails, data governance, and model hardening.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>通过巧妙的提示演示，揭示 Claude 在特定输入下可能泄露敏感信息的风险，凸显数据隐私与模型防护的重要性。文章提醒开发者加强 guardrails、数据治理和对模型的安全强化。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://ayush.digital/blog/the-memory-heist/opengraph-image' data-full='https://ayush.digital/blog/the-memory-heist/opengraph-image' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49086788' data-tags='Web,Career,Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49086788' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(110) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://elizabethtai.com/2026/06/10/substack-writers-you-need-a-website/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Substack writers, you need a website</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Substack 作家，你需要拥有一个个人网站</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49086788' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 637</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49086788' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 344</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Career'>Career</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49086788'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Relying on a platform like Substack can be limiting; owning a personal website gives creators control, SEO, and diversification of revenue streams. The note encourages Substack writers to steward their own online presence with simple, durable setups.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>仅依赖 Substack 容易受制于平台变动，拥有个人网站能为创作者带来更多控制权、搜索流量和收入来源。建议 Substack 作者建立简洁、可持续的自有站点来提升长期影响力。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i0.wp.com/elizabethtai.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/06/pexels-photo-16622632.jpeg?fit=1200%2C795&amp;ssl=1' data-full='https://i0.wp.com/elizabethtai.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/06/pexels-photo-16622632.jpeg?fit=1200%2C795&amp;ssl=1' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48819008' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(112) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.heise.de/en/news/Showdown-in-Strasbourg-The-unexpected-return-of-Chat-Control-1-0-11356680.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Chat Control passed first round in EU Parliament</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>欧盟议会完成对‘聊天控制’提案的首轮表决</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48819008' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 635</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48819008' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 259</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48819008'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The EU Parliament gave initial approval to the Chat Control proposal, advancing it to the next legislative stage. If enacted, it would mandate scanning private messages for abuse material, raising debates about end-to-end encryption, privacy, and platform obligations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>欧盟议会对‘聊天控制’提案完成初步表决，推进至下一轮立法程序。若最终通过，可能要求对私人消息进行扫描以发现虐待材料，引发对端对端加密、隐私保护及平台责任的广泛讨论。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://heise.cloudimg.io/bound/1200x1200/q85.png-lossy-85.webp-lossy-85.foil1/_www-heise-de_/imgs/18/5/1/1/2/7/6/7/shutterstock_2248568015-21aebc31fb107116.jpg' data-full='https://heise.cloudimg.io/bound/1200x1200/q85.png-lossy-85.webp-lossy-85.foil1/_www-heise-de_/imgs/18/5/1/1/2/7/6/7/shutterstock_2248568015-21aebc31fb107116.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(113) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.podman.io/2026/07/introducing-podman-v6-0-0/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Podman v6.0.0</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Podman 6.0.0 版本发布</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48762098' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 633</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48762098' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 248</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48762098'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Podman 6.0.0 marks a major milestone with expanded rootless container support, improved OCI compatibility, and streamlined tooling. It emphasizes security and developer ergonomics, aligning Podman with modern container workloads and Kubernetes workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>6.0.0 版本标志着 Podman 的一个重要里程碑，增强了无根容器支持、改进的 OCI 兼容性以及更简化的工具链。此版本强调安全性与开发者体验，使 Podman 更适合现代容器工作负载和 Kubernetes 场景。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48863464' data-tags='Legal,Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48863464' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(115) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theguardian.com/us-news/2026/jul/10/new-york-city-deceptive-subscriptions-ban' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>New York City to ban deceptive subscription practices</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>纽约市将禁止欺骗性订阅行为</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48863464' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 632</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48863464' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 334</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48863464'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>New York City plans to ban deceptive subscription practices, including auto-renewal traps and hidden charges. The move aims to improve consumer protection and force clearer disclosures from subscription-based services, potentially reshaping how onboarding and renewal terms are presented online. This could raise the bar for startups and platforms relying on subscription models.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>纽约市拟禁止欺骗性订阅行为，包括自动续订陷阱与隐藏费用，提升消费保护。此举将促使订阅型服务在用户注册与续订条款上提供更清晰的披露，或对采用订阅模式的企业带来影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i.guim.co.uk/img/media/b522a4563292b5972c9443aa2001a8637f1266b5/72_226_2591_2073/master/2591.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;quality=85&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=crop&amp;precrop=40:21,offset-x50,offset-y0&amp;overlay-align=bottom%2Cleft&amp;overlay-width=100p&amp;overlay-base64=L2ltZy9zdGF0aWMvb3ZlcmxheXMvdGctZGVmYXVsdC5wbmc&amp;enable=upscale&amp;s=8576dc8571bc0efaa7b0ef0eb44b88fa' data-full='https://i.guim.co.uk/img/media/b522a4563292b5972c9443aa2001a8637f1266b5/72_226_2591_2073/master/2591.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;quality=85&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=crop&amp;precrop=40:21,offset-x50,offset-y0&amp;overlay-align=bottom%2Cleft&amp;overlay-width=100p&amp;overlay-base64=L2ltZy9zdGF0aWMvb3ZlcmxheXMvdGctZGVmYXVsdC5wbmc&amp;enable=upscale&amp;s=8576dc8571bc0efaa7b0ef0eb44b88fa' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48747389' data-tags='Legal,Gaming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48747389' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(117) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://reclaimthenet.org/sony-deletes-551-studiocanal-movies-playstation-owners-paid-for' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Sony Deletes 551 Movies PlayStation Owners Paid For</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>索尼删除了 PlayStation 用户购买的 551 部电影</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48747389' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 627</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48747389' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 289</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48747389'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Sony reportedly removed 551 StudioCanal titles from PlayStation libraries, affecting owners who thought they had purchased those films. The incident underlines how digital ownership hinges on licensing and can disappear with rights changes. It raises questions about the permanence of digital libraries and consumer protections.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>据报导，索尼从 PlayStation 库中移除了 551 部 StudioCanal 的电影，影响了以为自己购买过的用户。事件凸显数字所有权依赖于许可，权利变更就可能导致内容消失。引发对数字馆藏永久性与消费者保护的思考。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://media.reclaimthenet.org/2026/06/uS2Yrsa3uRef.jpg' data-full='https://media.reclaimthenet.org/2026/06/uS2Yrsa3uRef.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(118) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/MaximeRivest/Riddle' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Fable turned reMarkable into Tom Riddle&#x27;s diary from Harry Potter</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>寓言将 reMarkable 打造成《哈利波特》中的汤姆·里德尔的日记</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48811591' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 622</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48811591' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 413</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Entertainment'>Entertainment</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48811591'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A playful project uses the Fable language to turn a reMarkable tablet into Tom Riddle&#x27;s diary from Harry Potter, blending code with a literary interface. It demonstrates how a programmable notebook can host narrative-driven experiences on niche hardware and invites creative experimentation with data-binding and UI.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个有趣的项目利用 Fable 将 reMarkable 平板打造成《哈利波特》里汤姆·里德尔的日记界面，结合编程与叙事界面。它展示了可编程笔记本在小众硬件上实现叙事体验的潜力，并鼓励在数据绑定与用户界面方面进行创意探索。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/bf557a54caacfbdc86dedfaa25067d0ff2f55612df34783c059bfc8c947af820/MaximeRivest/riddle' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/bf557a54caacfbdc86dedfaa25067d0ff2f55612df34783c059bfc8c947af820/MaximeRivest/riddle' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49034292' data-tags='Security,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49034292' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(119) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://hhh.hn/hanwha-github-token/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>My security camera shipped a GitHub admin token in its login page</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我的安防摄像头在登录页暴露了一个 GitHub 管理员令牌</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49034292' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 621</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49034292' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 205</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49034292'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A security camera shipped with a GitHub admin token embedded in its login page, potentially exposing credentials to anyone who visits. This incident highlights IoT authentication risks, insecure UI design, and the importance of secure firmware updates and token rotation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>安防摄像头的登录页暴露了一个 GitHub 管理员令牌，可能被任意访问者利用。这一事件凸显物联网认证风险、界面安全设计不足，以及对固件更新和令牌轮换的迫切需求。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/e/e7/Dynamic_Front_25_-_Finnish_Army_K9_Moukari_Self-Propelled_Howitzer_%288761382%29.jpg/1280px-Dynamic_Front_25_-_Finnish_Army_K9_Moukari_Self-Propelled_Howitzer_%288761382%29.jpg' data-full='https://upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/e/e7/Dynamic_Front_25_-_Finnish_Army_K9_Moukari_Self-Propelled_Howitzer_%288761382%29.jpg/1280px-Dynamic_Front_25_-_Finnish_Army_K9_Moukari_Self-Propelled_Howitzer_%288761382%29.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(121) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/immich-app/immich/discussions/29439' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Immich 3.0</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Immich 3.0 版本发布</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48761944' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 617</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48761944' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 287</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48761944'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Immich 3.0 ships major improvements to the open-source self-hosted photo backup app, including a refreshed UI, faster sync, and stronger privacy controls.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Immich 3.0 为开源自托管照片备份应用带来重大改进，包括焕然一新的界面、更快的同步速度以及更强的隐私控制。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49111237' data-tags='AI,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49111237' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(122) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://deepmind.google/blog/gemini-robotics-2-brings-whole-body-intelligence-to-robots/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Gemini Robotics 2 brings whole body intelligence to robots</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Gemini Robotics 2 将整身智能引入机器人</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49111237' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 611</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49111237' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 525</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49111237'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Gemini Robotics 2 expands whole-body intelligence for robots, coordinating sensing, planning, and actuation across the entire body. It leverages large-language-model-driven planning to orchestrate multi-limb manipulation and locomotion, enabling more flexible, goal-driven behavior in unstructured environments. This marks a step toward robots that can reason about tasks at a higher level rather than relying on hand-built controllers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Gemini Robotics 2 扩展了机器人整身智能，协同身体各部分进行感知、规划和执行。它利用基于大模型的规划来统一多肢的动作与移动，使机器人在非结构化环境中更具灵活性与目标导向。标志着让机器人以更高层次的任务推理来完成工作的一步。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://lh3.googleusercontent.com/VZ5KwQMxv9xBcQnYipsQB2EUj3oX1yvFYLktIamY8V2a76Y6ctEEuaLF59TuPdnaVn6OAMINDilqnuhju1O-AXc7QlOVmcogjskrWxS7xVQ1mc5S7g=w1200-h630-n-nu-rw' data-full='https://lh3.googleusercontent.com/VZ5KwQMxv9xBcQnYipsQB2EUj3oX1yvFYLktIamY8V2a76Y6ctEEuaLF59TuPdnaVn6OAMINDilqnuhju1O-AXc7QlOVmcogjskrWxS7xVQ1mc5S7g=w1200-h630-n-nu-rw' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49010167' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49010167' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(123) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/marcelroed/gigatoken/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>GigaToken: ~1000x faster Language model tokenization</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>GigaToken：语言模型分词速度提升约 1000 倍</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49010167' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 608</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49010167' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 119</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49010167'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>GigaToken claims tokenization speedups around 1000x for language models, potentially lowering preprocessing bottlenecks for large-scale deployments. If practical, it could shift cost and performance considerations for AI products that rely on tokenization as a bottleneck.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>GigaToken 声称能将语言模型分词速度提升约 1000×，显著降低大规模部署的预处理瓶颈。若落地可行，将改变依赖分词的 AI 产品的成本与性能权衡。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/24431f4e853971629dc9e63c9190d4a81121919ccd9d03ce6687563c917f1c4d/marcelroed/gigatoken' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/24431f4e853971629dc9e63c9190d4a81121919ccd9d03ce6687563c917f1c4d/marcelroed/gigatoken' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49103285' data-tags='AI,Startups'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49103285' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(124) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.science.org/content/article/ai-s-top-startups-are-barely-publishing-their-research' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AI&#x27;s top startups are barely publishing their research</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 的顶尖初创几乎不公开研究成果</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49103285' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 604</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49103285' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 315</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49103285'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Top AI startups publish little peer-reviewed research, preferring blog posts and press releases. This sparsity raises concerns about reproducibility, safety, and how quick advances are validated, as powerful models race ahead.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一些领先的 AI 初创公司公开的同行评审研究很少，更多依赖博客、公告。研究不透明可能影响结果复现与安全评估，也让快速迭代中的能力提升难以被独立验证。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48774518' data-tags='Open Source,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48774518' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(125) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.gamingonlinux.com/2026/07/valve-open-source-the-steam-machine-e-ink-screen-so-you-can-make-your-own/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Valve open-source the Steam Machine e-ink screen so you can make your own</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Valve 将 Steam Machine 的电子墨水屏开源，教你自制</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48774518' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 601</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48774518' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 114</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48774518'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Valve releases the e-ink display design from Steam Machine as open source, inviting creators to reuse and remix for their own projects. This openness lowers barriers for hardware experimentation and highlights a community driven approach to hardware prototyping.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Valve 将 Steam Machine 的电子墨水屏设计开源，鼓励创作者在自定义项目中复用与改造。这一开源举措降低了硬件试验门槛，凸显硬件原型设计的社区驱动性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.gamingonlinux.com/uploads/articles/tagline_images/130717818id29323gol_og.jpg' data-full='https://www.gamingonlinux.com/uploads/articles/tagline_images/130717818id29323gol_og.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49112867' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49112867' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(126) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://openai.com/index/advancing-the-price-performance-frontier-with-gpt-5-6/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Advancing the price-performance frontier with GPT‑5.6</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>以 GPT‑5.6 提升性价比前沿</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49112867' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 600</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49112867' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 394</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49112867'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI outlines how GPT-5.6 boosts price-performance through efficiency improvements and lower per-token costs. The upgrade could enable cheaper, faster AI inference for developers and businesses, widening access to advanced models.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI 介绍了 GPT‑5.6 在效率与成本方面的提升，降低每令牌成本，提升性价比。这一升级有望让开发者和企业以更低成本获得更快的推理能力，进一步普及高阶模型。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://images.ctfassets.net/kftzwdyauwt9/3YlohIkWAmTKqtreNc2Qwt/1ba93da6a8cb574aa0b299277874dbac/Frame__3_.png?w=1600&amp;h=900&amp;fit=fill' data-full='https://images.ctfassets.net/kftzwdyauwt9/3YlohIkWAmTKqtreNc2Qwt/1ba93da6a8cb574aa0b299277874dbac/Frame__3_.png?w=1600&amp;h=900&amp;fit=fill' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49126604' data-tags='Open Source,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49126604' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(127) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/yc-software/qm' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>qm – Multiplayer agent harness for work</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>qm：面向工作场景的多人代理框架</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49126604' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 596</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49126604' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 127</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49126604'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>qm is an open-source harness that coordinates multiple agents to work on tasks collaboratively. It provides orchestration, tasking, and visibility across agent-driven workflows, helping teams tackle complex problems more efficiently.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>qm 是一个开源的多人代理协调框架，支持在工作场景中让多个代理协同完成任务。它提供编排、任务分发和对代理驱动工作流的可视化，帮助团队更高效地解决复杂问题。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/6829f6872e88bca87e7d85c7d6497b3246daac80d02fc34591f8b1e055875e33/yc-software/qm' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/6829f6872e88bca87e7d85c7d6497b3246daac80d02fc34591f8b1e055875e33/yc-software/qm' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49089755' data-tags='AI,Open Source,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49089755' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(128) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/openai/codex-security' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Codex Security</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>OpenAI 将 Codex Security 开源</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49089755' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 591</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49089755' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 226</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49089755'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI released Codex Security as an open-source project, inviting security researchers to inspect and improve the security of Codex-powered tooling. The project provides tooling for auditing prompts, monitoring outputs, and identifying vulnerabilities across code-generation pipelines. By open-sourcing it, OpenAI accelerates community-driven security research and invites broader scrutiny of AI-enabled coding systems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI 将 Codex Security 开源，面向安全研究者提供对 Codex 相关工具的审计与改进能力。该项目支持对 prompts、输出和代码生成流程中的潜在漏洞进行分析，并提升对 AI 辅助编码系统的可审计性。此举可能加速社区在 AI 安全领域的研究与防护工具普及。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/4f6065cf6e0c5afd7891abd9f6003e14e6ebf5695f62bda7c552726a77337532/openai/codex-security' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/4f6065cf6e0c5afd7891abd9f6003e14e6ebf5695f62bda7c552726a77337532/openai/codex-security' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48962983' data-tags='Privacy,Legal,AI,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48962983' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(129) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://petapixel.com/2026/07/16/mayor-mamdani-says-landlords-cant-secretly-use-ai-images-to-advertise-properties/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>NYC may require landlords and realtors to disclose the use of AI in listings</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>纽约市可能要求房东披露在房源信息中使用 AI 的情况</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48962983' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 581</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48962983' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 262</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48962983'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>New York City is considering regulations to require landlords and real estate agents to disclose the use of AI-generated imagery in listings. The move aims to curb deceptive advertising and increase transparency in online property listings, with implications for proptech platforms and compliance burdens.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>纽约市正考虑立法，要求房东和中介披露房源照片中是否使用了 AI 生成的图像。此举旨在提升在线房源广告的透明度，影响房地产科技平台的合规成本与运营方式。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://petapixel.com/assets/uploads/2026/07/mamdani-copy.jpg' data-full='https://petapixel.com/assets/uploads/2026/07/mamdani-copy.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49043724' data-tags='Science,Education'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49043724' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(130) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.maths.cam.ac.uk/features/professor-hannah-fry-wins-leelavati-prize' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Hannah Fry Wins the Leelavati Prize in 2026 for Mathematics Outreach</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>汉娜·弗莱获2026年 Leelavati 数学传播奖</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49043724' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 581</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49043724' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 109</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49043724'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Hannah Fry is awarded the Leelavati Prize for mathematics outreach, recognizing her work turning complex math ideas into accessible public-facing content. The award underscores the value of science communication in expanding participation in STEM and inspires broader engagement with mathematical ideas.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>汉娜·弗莱因其将复杂数学观念转化为易于大众理解的内容而获颁 Leelavati 数学传播奖，肯定了她在科学传播方面的贡献。该奖项强调了公众沟通在扩大 STEM 参与度方面的重要性，并激励更多人关注与学习数学。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48957779' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48957779' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(131) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://old.reddit.com/r/math/comments/1uxj3cy/after_openais_cdc_proof_announcement_gpt56_used_a/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>GPT-5.6 used a prompt to close a 30-year gap in convex optimization</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>GPT-5.6 用提示弥补凸优化三十年难题</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48957779' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 580</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48957779' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 380</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48957779'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A prompt reportedly closed a 30-year gap in convex optimization after a CDC-style proof announcement. If verified, it demonstrates how carefully crafted prompts can guide AI to tackle deep mathematical problems and produce testable steps. The claim invites scrutiny of reproducibility and the proper role of AI in formal proofs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>据称，GPT-5.6 通过一个提示，解决了凸优化领域长达30年的未解难题，这一成果在 CDC 风格证明公告后出现。若属实，表明大型语言模型在帮助人类数学家进行问题表述和证据推导方面具有潜在价值，同时也需要对可重复性与独立验证保持警惕。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Claude Code has been reworked to run on Bun, with the runtime implemented in Rust. The change promises faster startup and reduced overhead, potentially broadening compatibility and enabling smoother integration with Rust-native tooling.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Claude Code 已改为在 Bun 运行，运行时以 Rust 实现。这一改动有望提升启动速度和性能开销，便于与 Rust 原生工具链的集成。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(135) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://unsung.aresluna.org/if-youre-a-button-you-have-one-job/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>If you&#x27;re a button, you have one job</a></p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A provocative take on the role of a button: it has one job—to trigger actions. The piece argues for proper semantics and accessibility, urging developers to reserve button elements for actions and use links where appropriate.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章强调按钮的本质是执行操作，应正确使用按钮元素以提升可访问性与可预测性，避免将用于导航的元素错用成按钮。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(137) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://tailscale.com/blog/hugging-face-intrusion' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Tailscale didn&#x27;t stop the Hugging Face intrusion</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Tailscale 未阻止 Hugging Face 入侵</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49127306' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 566</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49127306' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 211</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49127306'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Hugging Face disclosed a security incident in which attackers bypassed protections despite Tailscale, underscoring that zero-trust networking is not a sole defense. The post details how leaked credentials enabled access and the lessons about defense-in-depth and secret hygiene.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Hugging Face 公开了一起安全事件，攻击者在使用 Tailscale 的情况下仍然进入内部系统，说明零信任网络并非万用钥匙。文中描述了凭证泄露如何促成入侵，并强调需要多层防护与良好密钥管理。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48755731' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(138) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://tedium.co/2026/07/01/online-web-forums-retrospective/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Bring back crappy forums</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>让糟糕论坛卷土重来</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48755731' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 564</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48755731' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 347</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48755731'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A retrospective argues for reviving the rough-and-tumble forum culture that predates modern platforms—less polish, more candid discussion, and simpler moderation. It examines what early forums did well and what modern networks could borrow, from lightweight threading to signal-to-noise management.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>该文回顾并主张让糟糕但充满活力的论坛文化回归——强调直率讨论、低门槛与简化的社区治理。它讨论早期论坛的优点，以及现代社群应借鉴的经验，如简化的主题讨论与信号噪声管理。</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>CarPlay 是增补而非替代</p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48769397'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The author contends that CarPlay augments existing car interfaces rather than replacing them, adding convenience without solving deeper UX and safety trade-offs in automotive software.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者认为 CarPlay 是对车载界面的补充，而非替代，带来便利但并未解决汽车软件中的深层 UX 与安全权衡。文章强调苹果的策略改变了用户预期和生态格局，迫使厂商在整合与保留车辆专属控制间寻找平衡。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49031796' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49031796' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(141) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://bfl.ai/blog/flux-3' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Flux 3</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Flux 3 版本更新</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49031796' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 562</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49031796' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 132</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49031796'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Flux 3 marks the next major version of Flux.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Flux 3 标志着 Flux 的下一重大版本。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://bfl.ai/api/og/blog/flux-3' data-full='https://bfl.ai/api/og/blog/flux-3' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48920181' data-tags='Finance,Business,Space'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48920181' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(142) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.ft.com/content/3a023b95-66c3-41e1-b0ce-df752a499541' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>SpaceX bond worth 10% less than issue price – heading for junk bond status</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>SpaceX 债券发行价下跌 10%：有望走向垃圾债评级</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48920181' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 560</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48920181' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 581</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Space'>Space</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48920181'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>SpaceX bonds trade roughly 10% below issue price, signaling waning investor demand or rising perceived risk. If the debt slides toward junk status, SpaceX could face higher refinancing costs and tighter liquidity windows as macro headwinds bite tech funding. This serves as a reminder that even highly popular tech issuers are not immune to debt-market sentiment.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>SpaceX 债券交易价格约较发行价低约10%，反映市场对其风险的重新定价或需求走弱。若债务评级进一步恶化，再融资成本将上升，流动性可能收紧，凸显当前宏观环境对科技股相关债务的冲击。此事再次印证即使是备受关注的科技发行也会受债市情绪影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://images.ft.com/v3/image/raw/https%3A%2F%2Fimages.ft.com%2Fv3%2Fimage%2Fraw%2Fhttps%3A%2F%2Fd1e00ek4ebabms.cloudfront.net%2Fproduction%2F6c6b2f96-a449-466f-8e52-c2a73ea583ed.jpg%3Fsource%3Dnext-barrier-page%26fit%3Dscale-down%26quality%3Dhighest%26width%3D700%26dpr%3D1?source=next-barrier-page' data-full='https://images.ft.com/v3/image/raw/https%3A%2F%2Fimages.ft.com%2Fv3%2Fimage%2Fraw%2Fhttps%3A%2F%2Fd1e00ek4ebabms.cloudfront.net%2Fproduction%2F6c6b2f96-a449-466f-8e52-c2a73ea583ed.jpg%3Fsource%3Dnext-barrier-page%26fit%3Dscale-down%26quality%3Dhighest%26width%3D700%26dpr%3D1?source=next-barrier-page' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49007374' data-tags='Security,Design'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49007374' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(143) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://twitter.com/nikitabier/status/2079787406300266743' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Passkeys were invented by engineers with zero understanding of consumer brain</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Passkeys 由对用户认知不足的工程师发明</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49007374' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 560</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49007374' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 778</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49007374'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Passkeys aim to replace passwords with cryptographic credentials, but critics argue the UX reflects engineers’ blind spots about how real users think. Adoption friction could hobble the technology unless UX and recovery flows are rethought.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Passkeys 被视为替代密码的安全升级，但有人批评其用户体验暴露了工程师对普通用户认知的盲点。若不改进 UX 与恢复流程，普及应用将面临更大阻力。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://pbs.twimg.com/profile_images/1755448801957945344/Fh2HNw5Y_200x200.jpg' data-full='https://pbs.twimg.com/profile_images/1755448801957945344/Fh2HNw5Y_200x200.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48894752' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48894752' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(144) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://get-inscribe.com/blog/apple-speech-api-benchmark.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Apple&#x27;s new SpeechAnalyzer API, benchmarked against Whisper and its predecessor</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>苹果新 SpeechAnalyzer API 与 Whisper 对比基准</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48894752' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 560</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48894752' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 234</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48894752'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Apple&#x27;s new SpeechAnalyzer API is benchmarked against Whisper and Apple&#x27;s own older speech API. The results highlight on-device performance, latency, and accuracy tradeoffs, offering insights into when to choose cloud vs. on-device processing for voice apps.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>苹果新推出的 SpeechAnalyzer API 经过与 Whisper 及前代 API 的对比基准测试，凸显了设备端与云端推理的权衡。评测聚焦准确率、延迟和噪声鲁棒性，为构建本地化语音应用提供实用参考。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1589254065878-42c9da997008?w=1200&amp;h=600&amp;fit=crop&amp;q=80' data-full='https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1589254065878-42c9da997008?w=1200&amp;h=600&amp;fit=crop&amp;q=80' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48989701' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(145) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://qwen.ai/blog?id=qwen-image-3.0' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Qwen-Image-3.0: Rich Content, Authentic Details, Deep Knowledge</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Qwen-Image-3.0：丰富内容、真实细节与深厚知识</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48989701' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 559</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48989701' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 213</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48989701'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Qwen-Image-3.0 showcases richer content, authentic details, and deeper knowledge in its multimodal capabilities. The update promises improved image generation with factual grounding and better contextual reasoning, appealing to designers, educators, and analysts. This signals a step toward more trustworthy AI-assisted visual content.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Qwen-Image-3.0 展现出更丰富的内容、更真实的细节，以及更深的知识储备，提升了多模态能力。更新强调在生成图像时的事实性与上下文推理，适用于设计师、教育工作者等对视觉内容可靠性有高要求的场景。这标志着 AI 辅助视觉内容可信度的提升。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://img.alicdn.com/imgextra/i4/O1CN01OXv3EM1FN8t9W4P79_!!6000000000474-2-tps-80-80.png' data-full='https://img.alicdn.com/imgextra/i4/O1CN01OXv3EM1FN8t9W4P79_!!6000000000474-2-tps-80-80.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48735770' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(146) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://claude.com/product/claude-science' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Claude Science</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Claude Science</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48735770' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 559</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48735770' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 173</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48735770'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Claude Science is a new product focused on scientific workloads, offering specialized reasoning and data-handling features for researchers. It aims to speed up literature review, modeling, and experimentation workflows for scientists.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Claude Science 是面向科学工作负载的新产品，提供专门的推理和数据处理能力，帮助研究人员加速分析与建模。面向研究人员和数据科学家，旨在优化文献综述、实验设计与数据分析的工作流。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/6889473510b50328dbb70ae6/6a429b9909aa1b063a203021_life-sciences-tn.jpg' data-full='https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/6889473510b50328dbb70ae6/6a429b9909aa1b063a203021_life-sciences-tn.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49005747' data-tags='Web,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49005747' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(147) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.cole-k.com/2026/07/21/reddit/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>So Reddit has decided that plain HTML is unsafe</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Reddit 竟然认定纯 HTML 不再安全</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49005747' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 558</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49005747' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 573</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49005747'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Describes Reddit’s move to treat plain HTML as unsafe content, aiming to curb misuse and scripting abuse. The change affects developers relying on HTML embeds and has implications for moderation and third-party tools.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>描述 Reddit 将纯 HTML 视为不安全内容的举措，意在遏制滥用与脚本滥用。这一变动影响依赖 HTML 嵌入的开发者，并对治理和第三方工具带来影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.cole-k.com/img/reddit/old-reddit-login-short.png' data-full='https://www.cole-k.com/img/reddit/old-reddit-login-short.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48776044' data-tags='Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48776044' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(148) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://phenomenalworld.org/analysis/the-anti-amazon/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Costco is the anti-Amazon</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Costco：与亚马逊截然不同的商业模式</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48776044' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 556</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48776044' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 556</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48776044'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Explores how Costco’s membership model, bulk-buying, limited SKUs, and disciplined logistics create a different kind of customer loyalty and price discipline than Amazon’s marketplace approach. The piece argues these choices position Costco as the anti-Amazon in the modern retail era.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章分析 Costco 的会员制、批量采购、有限 SKU 和严密的物流管理，展示其与亚马逊的不同商业模式与竞争优势，称其为现代零售中的‘反亚马逊’。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://phenomenalworld.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/Copy-of-2024-NEW-PW-Twitter-Vertical-Article-Preview-8.png' data-full='https://phenomenalworld.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/Copy-of-2024-NEW-PW-Twitter-Vertical-Article-Preview-8.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(149) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://scotto.me/blog/2026-07-12-how-to-read-more-books/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How to read more books</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>如何读更多的书</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48882056' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 556</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48882056' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 290</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48882056'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Practical strategies to increase reading volume: set goals, schedule time, annotate actively, and curate reading lists to sustain momentum.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>提升阅读量的实用策略包括设定目标、安排时间、主动标注以及精心管理阅读清单，以持续保持阅读势头。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(150) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://apnews.com/article/ai-anthropic-copyright-settlement-claude-books-bartz-74b140444023898aeba8579b6e9f0d63' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Judge approves $1.5B Anthropic settlement for pirated books used to train Claude</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>法官批准 15 亿美元 Anthropic 诉讼和解，涉盗版书用于训练 Claude</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48996652' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 554</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48996652' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 593</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48996652'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Judge approves a $1.5B settlement over pirate books used to train Claude, highlighting copyright risk in AI training data.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>法官批准 15 亿美元的Anthropic 诉讼和解，涉及将盗版书用于训练 Claude 的问题，凸显 AI 训练数据中的版权风险。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48744518' data-tags='Open Source,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48744518' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(151) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://asahilinux.org/2026/06/progress-report-7-1/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Asahi Linux 7.1 Progress Report</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Asahi Linux 7.1 进展报告</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48744518' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 553</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48744518' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 210</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48744518'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Asahi Linux 7.1 delivers new kernel improvements, broader Apple Silicon support, and performance tweaks aimed at making Macs run Linux more smoothly. The update narrows hardware gaps and enhances stability for everyday use and development on Apple hardware. It signals ongoing momentum for Linux on consumer devices.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Asahi Linux 7.1 的进展包括内核改进、对 Apple Silicon 的更广泛支持以及性能优化，旨在让在 Mac 上运行 Linux 更加顺畅。此次更新缩小了硬件兼容性差距、提升了日常使用与开发的稳定性，继续推动 Linux 在消费级设备上的应用。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://asahilinux.org/img/AsahiLinux_logomark_256px.png' data-full='https://asahilinux.org/img/AsahiLinux_logomark_256px.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48847552' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48952880' data-tags='AI,Health,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48952880' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(153) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://localnewsmatters.org/2026/07/15/kaiser-nurses-say-ai-workplace-surveillance-are-making-their-jobs-and-patient-care-worse/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Kaiser nurses say AI, surveillance are making their jobs and patient care worse</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>凯撒医疗系统护士称 AI 与监控让工作和病人护理变得更糟</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48952880' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 552</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48952880' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 372</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48952880'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Frontline nurses report that AI decision-support and surveillance systems add administrative burden and impair patient interaction, potentially harming care quality. The piece underscores calls for better governance, transparency, and careful deployment of automation in healthcare.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一线护士表示人工智能决策支持和工作场所监控增加了工作负担，影响与病人互动，可能损及护理质量。文章强调需要更好的治理、透明度，以及在医疗领域更谨慎地部署自动化。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i0.wp.com/localnewsmatters.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/LNM-20260713-MATTERSKAISER-01.jpg?fit=2000%2C1333&amp;ssl=1' data-full='https://i0.wp.com/localnewsmatters.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/LNM-20260713-MATTERSKAISER-01.jpg?fit=2000%2C1333&amp;ssl=1' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(154) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/eu-digital-identity-wallet/av-doc-technical-specification/discussions/19' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>European &quot;age verification&quot; &quot;app&quot; forcing everyone to use Android or iOS</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>欧洲的年龄验证应用强制使用 Android 或 iOS</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48903777' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 551</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48903777' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 395</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48903777'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>European age-verification discussions reveal a regulatory push that effectively forces users onto mobile platforms, raising concerns about privacy, interoperability, and accessibility.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>关于欧洲年龄验证应用的讨论揭示了一个监管推动，实质上强制用户依赖 Android 或 iOS，带来隐私、互操作性与可访问性方面的担忧。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/763f5184b61bd0127a10c1cddee3e05d6f53474eab84ed816cd0a86b9be9267a/eu-digital-identity-wallet/av-doc-technical-specification/discussions/19' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/763f5184b61bd0127a10c1cddee3e05d6f53474eab84ed816cd0a86b9be9267a/eu-digital-identity-wallet/av-doc-technical-specification/discussions/19' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48911793' data-tags='AI,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48911793' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(155) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/moonshine-ai/moonshine/tree/main/micro' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Speech Recognition and TTS in less than 500kb</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>不到500KB的語音識別與文字轉語音</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48911793' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 547</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48911793' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 82</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48911793'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Ultra-low-footprint speech recognition and TTS can run in under 500KB, enabling offline voice interfaces on microcontrollers and other constrained devices. The project highlights design choices that trade off accuracy for size, with potential uses in privacy-preserving, edge AI.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>極低資源的語音識別與文字轉語音能在不足500KB內運行，使微控制器和受限設備具備離線語音輸入/輸出能力。該項目展示了為了縮小尺寸而進行的設計取捨，對注重隱私的邊緣AI場景尤具吸引力。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://repository-images.githubusercontent.com/867866058/0fd49719-61f4-4a3d-bb6f-eda467526a0c' data-full='https://repository-images.githubusercontent.com/867866058/0fd49719-61f4-4a3d-bb6f-eda467526a0c' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49015639' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(156) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://simonwillison.net/2026/Jul/22/openai-cyberattack/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>OpenAI’s accidental attack against Hugging Face is science fiction that happened</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>OpenAI 的意外攻击对 Hugging Face 就是真实的科幻</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49015639' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 546</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49015639' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 418</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49015639'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An analysis arguing that OpenAI&#x27;s accidental cyber attack on Hugging Face mirrors a sci-fi security scenario playing out in the real world, highlighting cross-platform security risks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>分析认为 OpenAI 误发的针对 Hugging Face 的网络攻击如同现实中上演的科幻式安全场景，凸显跨平台安全风险。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(157) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.pcgamer.com/gaming-industry/open-source-game-engine-godot-will-no-longer-accept-ai-authored-code-contributions-we-cant-trust-heavy-users-of-ai-to-understand-their-code-enough-to-fix-it/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Godot will no longer accept AI-authored code contributions</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Godot 将不再接受 AI 编写的代码贡献</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48743472' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 544</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48743472' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 387</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48743472'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Godot Foundation bans AI-authored code contributions to the core, citing concerns about reliability and maintainability. The policy underscores the ongoing tension in OSS between AI-assisted tooling and the need for human oversight in critical code.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Godot 基金会宣布不再接受 AI 编写的代码提交，理由是对可靠性和可维护性的担忧。此举凸显开源社区在 AI 辅助编码和人类审阅之间的持续张力。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.mos.cms.futurecdn.net/9fe6i3bvMxcRA6sb2TJAS-1600-80.jpg' data-full='https://cdn.mos.cms.futurecdn.net/9fe6i3bvMxcRA6sb2TJAS-1600-80.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48897945'>

<p class='hn-title'>(159) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://jdan.github.io/98.css/#status-bar' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>98.css</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>98.css：让网页重现 Windows 98 风格的 CSS 框架</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49028927' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 543</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49028927' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 124</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49028927'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>98.css is a tiny CSS framework that recreates Windows 98-era UI with modern HTML. It enables quick retro-style demos and nostalgia-driven UI experiments without heavy dependencies. The project showcases how small CSS kits can deliver a complete desktop-like aesthetic with just a few lines.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>98.css 是一个小型 CSS 框架，能够把网页渲染成 Windows 98 时代的界面。它便于快速做出复古风格的仪表板或演示，而无需繁重的依赖。该项目展示了极简 CSS 组件也能实现桌面级美观的效果。</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>无需打开 Xcode 就能构建与发布 Mac 与 iOS 应用</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48896665' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 542</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48896665' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 230</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48896665'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Describes a workflow to build and ship Mac and iOS apps without ever opening Xcode, relying on CLI tools and automation. It highlights how signing, packaging, and deployment can be automated in CI/CD, reducing GUI-focused work.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>介绍了一条无需打开 Xcode 就能构建和发布 Mac 与 iOS 应用的工作流，依赖命令行工具与自动化。强调在 CI/CD 中自动化签名、打包与部署，减少对 GUI 的依赖。</p>
