<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49120097' data-tags='AI,Security,Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49120097' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(162) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.google/security/chrome-stronger-with-every-update/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Google fixed more Chrome bugs in June than over the past two years, thanks to AI</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>谷歌靠 AI，在六月修复的 Chrome 漏洞数量超过过去两年总和</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49120097' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 540</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49120097' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 559</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49120097'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Google credits AI-assisted triage and patch generation for fixing more Chrome bugs in June than in the previous two years combined. This shows how AI tooling can accelerate vulnerability response and elevate browser hardening. It could foreshadow broader shifts in security workflows across software teams.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>谷歌称借助 AI 的分流与补丁生成，六月修复的 Chrome 漏洞数量超过过去两年的总和。这显示出 AI 工具能加速漏洞响应、提升浏览器安全性。此举或预示软件团队在安全工作流程上的更广泛变革。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://storage.googleapis.com/gweb-uniblog-publish-prod/images/Screenshot_2026-07-17_at_5.00.43PM.width-1300.png' data-full='https://storage.googleapis.com/gweb-uniblog-publish-prod/images/Screenshot_2026-07-17_at_5.00.43PM.width-1300.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48745113' data-tags='Business,Gaming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48745113' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(163) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://mynintendonews.com/2026/06/26/nintendo-has-raised-its-employees-base-salary-by-10/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Nintendo has raised its employees base salary by 10%</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>任天堂将员工基本薪资提高10%</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48745113' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 535</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48745113' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 326</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48745113'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Nintendo announced a 10% raise to base salaries, signaling wage growth in a major entertainment tech company. The move may aid retention and morale amid tight labor markets while squeezing margins modestly. It also mirrors broader wage-tightening and inflation-adaptation trends in the industry.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>任天堂宣布将员工基本工资提高10%，显示出在劳动力紧张的市场中公司对人才的重视。此举有助于提升留任与士气，但可能对利润率产生一定挤压，反映出行业的薪资调整与通胀应对趋势。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i0.wp.com/mynintendonews.com/wp-content/uploads/2024/09/nintendo_logo.jpg?fit=2048%2C1341&amp;ssl=1' data-full='https://i0.wp.com/mynintendonews.com/wp-content/uploads/2024/09/nintendo_logo.jpg?fit=2048%2C1341&amp;ssl=1' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48770647' data-tags='Politics,Business,DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48770647' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(164) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://stefan.schueller.net/posts/the-free-market-lie/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Why Switzerland has 25 gbit internet and America doesn&#x27;t</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>自由市场的谬误：为何瑞士有25 Gbit互联网而美国没有</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48770647' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 534</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48770647' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 429</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48770647'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues that Switzerland’s fast, ubiquitous fiber is the result of deliberate public policy, infrastructure sharing, and local competition rather than a laissez-faire telecom market. It contrasts that with structural barriers in the U.S.—incumbent incentives, pole and rights-of-way issues, and regulatory choices—that have kept speeds and deployment lagging despite market rhetoric.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章指出，瑞士的高速光纤来自有意的公共政策、基础设施共享和地方性竞争，而不是放任的自由市场。与之相对，美国因既得利益、线路与通行权问题及监管选择，导致部署和速率长期落后。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://stefan.schueller.net/posts/the-free-market-lie/title-image.png' data-full='https://stefan.schueller.net/posts/the-free-market-lie/title-image.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48763035' data-tags='Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48763035' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(165) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://mathstodon.xyz/@iblech/116769502749142438' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Since Linux 6.9, LUKS suspend stopped wiping disk-encryption keys from memory</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>自 Linux 6.9 起，LUKS 休眠不再从内存擦除磁盘加密密钥</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48763035' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 531</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48763035' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 222</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48763035'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Linux 6.9 introduced a change where LUKS suspend no longer clears in-memory keys, trading security for faster resume. The decision highlights the ongoing tension between safeguarding encryption keys and preserving usability in sleep states.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>自 Linux 6.9 起，LUKS 休眠不再从内存中清除磁盘加密密钥，提升恢复速度的同时扩大了内存中密钥暴露的风险。这一取舍凸显了在安全性与可用性之间的持续权衡。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://mathstodon.xyz/packs/assets/logo-DXQkHAe5.svg' data-full='https://mathstodon.xyz/packs/assets/logo-DXQkHAe5.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48947560' data-tags='Science,Energy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48947560' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(166) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bbc.com/news/articles/cy4kdd1e0ejo' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>First atmosphere found on Earth-like planet in habitable zone of distant star</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在遥远恒星的宜居带首次发现地球样行星的大气</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48947560' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 529</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48947560' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 304</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48947560'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Astronomers detected atmospheric signals on an Earth-like planet in a distant star’s habitable zone, marking a milestone in characterizing rocky worlds beyond our solar system. The finding invigorates plans for future telescopes to search for biosignatures and assess habitability on exoplanets.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>天文学家在遥远恒星的宜居带内探测到地球样行星的大气信号，标志着对系外岩石行星表征的重大进展。此发现将推动未来望远镜在星际尺度上寻找生物征兆并评估宜居性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://ichef.bbci.co.uk/news/1024/branded_news/2cd8/live/f3f60290-81d1-11f1-926f-c90d1bcfbc84.jpg' data-full='https://ichef.bbci.co.uk/news/1024/branded_news/2cd8/live/f3f60290-81d1-11f1-926f-c90d1bcfbc84.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(167) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://xn--gckvb8fzb.com/i-regret-migrating-to-codeberg/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I regret migrating to Codeberg</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49021856' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 528</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49021856' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 529</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49021856'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A personal critique of migrating to Codeberg, weighing issues like reliability, community, and tools against the allure of alternative platforms.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者分享迁移到 Codeberg 的个人体验，权衡可靠性、社区生态与工具链等因素，与其他平台的优劣进行比较。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49038060' data-tags='AI,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49038060' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(168) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theguardian.com/technology/2026/jul/24/openai-rogue-hacker' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Be skeptical of OpenAI&#x27;s rogue hacker agent story</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>对 OpenAI 的 rogue hacker agent 故事保持怀疑</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49038060' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 527</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49038060' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 294</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49038060'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Questioning the veracity of a reported OpenAI rogue hacker agent incident, the piece argues for skepticism and better verification in AI security reporting. It discusses how sensational stories can mislead about AI autonomy and security risk.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章对关于 OpenAI 失控黑客代理的报道保持怀疑，强调需要更严格的核验与背景信息。讨论此类新闻对 AI 安全感知的影响以及报道方式的影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i.guim.co.uk/img/media/e3c91fad3c0af25a6cbbd44c6b091bdfdfdd270d/952_0_4448_3558/master/4448.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;quality=85&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=crop&amp;precrop=40:21,offset-x50,offset-y0&amp;overlay-align=bottom%2Cleft&amp;overlay-width=100p&amp;overlay-base64=L2ltZy9zdGF0aWMvb3ZlcmxheXMvdGctb3BpbmlvbnMucG5n&amp;enable=upscale&amp;s=d8a12eef6083a120c19a454c8d40fcfd' data-full='https://i.guim.co.uk/img/media/e3c91fad3c0af25a6cbbd44c6b091bdfdfdd270d/952_0_4448_3558/master/4448.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;quality=85&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=crop&amp;precrop=40:21,offset-x50,offset-y0&amp;overlay-align=bottom%2Cleft&amp;overlay-width=100p&amp;overlay-base64=L2ltZy9zdGF0aWMvb3ZlcmxheXMvdGctb3BpbmlvbnMucG5n&amp;enable=upscale&amp;s=d8a12eef6083a120c19a454c8d40fcfd' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49036433' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(169) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.thehindu.com/news/national/government-orders-github-to-remove-bluetooth-based-chat-app-bitchat-over-security-concerns-jack-dorsey/article71262049.ece' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Government orders GitHub to remove Bluetooth-based chat app Bitchat: Jack Dorsey</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>政府要求 GitHub 下架蓝牙聊天应用 Bitchat，涉及安全担忧</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49036433' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 526</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49036433' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 431</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49036433'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A government directive orders removal of a Bluetooth-based chat app linked to a high-profile figure, citing security concerns. The case highlights how policy actions intersect with platform hosting and privacy in distributed apps.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>政府指令要求下架与高知名人物相关的蓝牙聊天应用，因安全担忧而采取行动。此事凸显政策行为与平台托管、隐私在分布式应用场景中的交叉影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://th-i.thgim.com/public/incoming/s017lf/article71262085.ece/alternates/LANDSCAPE_1200/12-11-JACK_DORSEY_CEO_TWITTER_B.jpg' data-full='https://th-i.thgim.com/public/incoming/s017lf/article71262085.ece/alternates/LANDSCAPE_1200/12-11-JACK_DORSEY_CEO_TWITTER_B.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48966713' data-tags='Hardware,Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48966713' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(170) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://chipweinberger.com/articles/20260719-hardware-is-not-so-hard' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>What I learned selling 2,500 MIDI recorders: Hardware is not so hard</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>卖出2500个 MIDI 记录器让我明白硬件并不难</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48966713' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 525</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48966713' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 238</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48966713'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The author shares hands-on lessons from selling 2,500 MIDI recorders, arguing that hardware development is more approachable than it seems with smart sourcing, tooling, and customer feedback loops. Expect practical notes on prototyping, cost control, manufacturing, and post-sale support for indie hardware projects.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者在销售2500个 MIDI 记录器的过程中总结了实战经验，认为在正确的 sourcing、工具链和客户反馈循环下，硬件开发并不如想象中那么难。文章提供从原型、成本控制到制造与售后支持的实用要点，适合独立硬件创业者借鉴。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://chipweinberger.com/assets/20260719-hardware-is-not-so-hard/jamcorder-in-situ.png' data-full='https://chipweinberger.com/assets/20260719-hardware-is-not-so-hard/jamcorder-in-situ.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48827858' data-tags='AI,Security,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48827858' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(171) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://noma.security/blog/gitlost-how-we-tricked-githubs-ai-agent-into-leaking-private-repos/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>GitLost: We Tricked GitHub&#x27;s AI Agent into Leaking Private Repos</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>GitLost：我们欺骗 GitHub 的 AI 代理泄露私有仓库</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48827858' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 525</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48827858' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 199</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48827858'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers show a vulnerability where an AI-powered assistant on GitHub can be coaxed into exposing private repositories. By manipulating prompts and data flows, they demonstrate leakage risks from copilots and automated tooling. This underscores the need for stronger data-handling controls, better prompt safeguards, and auditability in AI-assisted development ecosystems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究者展示了一种漏洞：GitHub 上的 AI 助手在被引导下可能泄露私有仓库的内容。通过操纵提示和数据流，他们演示了协作工具与自动化流程中的数据泄露风险。这凸显了在 AI 辅助开发生态中加强数据处理、提示防护和审计机制的重要性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://noma.security/wp-content/uploads/Screenshot-2026-06-29-at-14.16.35.png' data-full='https://noma.security/wp-content/uploads/Screenshot-2026-06-29-at-14.16.35.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48768951' data-tags='Privacy,Legal,AI,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48768951' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(172) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://righttointelligence.org/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Protect your right to run local AI</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>保护本地运行 AI 的权利</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48768951' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 524</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48768951' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 185</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48768951'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Campaigns argue users should be allowed to run AI models locally, offline, on their own hardware without cloud dependence. That shift would boost privacy, data control, and resilience, though hardware costs and licensing remain barriers. The piece frames why this matters to developers and enthusiasts who want autonomy over their AI tools.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>倡导者认为人们应能在本地硬件上离线运行 AI 模型，避免对云端的依赖。这一转变能提升隐私、数据掌控和系统韧性，但硬件成本和许可证限制仍是现实障碍。文章聚焦对于希望掌控 AI 工具的开发者和爱好者为何重要。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://righttointelligence.org/og.png' data-full='https://righttointelligence.org/og.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48863490' data-tags='AI,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48863490' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(173) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://cdn.openai.com/pdf/04d1d1e4-bc75-476a-97cf-49055cd98d31/cdc_proof.pdf' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>GPT-5.6 Sol Ultra produces proof of the Cycle Double Cover Conjecture [pdf]</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>GPT-5.6 Sol Ultra 产出循环双覆盖猜想的证明（PDF）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48863490' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 523</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48863490' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 429</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48863490'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>GPT-5.6 Sol Ultra has produced a proof of the Cycle Double Cover Conjecture, with a PDF provided. If verified, this would mark a notable milestone in AI-assisted formal proofs and could accelerate future mathematical work, but the proof will be rigorously scrutinized by the community. The episode highlights AI&#x27;s potential to aid deep mathematics while underscoring the need for careful validation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>GPT-5.6 Sol Ultra 似乎给出了循环双覆盖猜想的证明，并附有 PDF。若经同行验证，这一成果可能标志着 AI 辅助形式化证明迈出重要一步，或加速后续的数学研究，但证明将接受严格审查。此事凸显 AI 在高深数学领域的潜力，同时也强调需要谨慎验证。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48858121' data-tags='Design'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48858121' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(174) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.gingerbill.org/article/2026/07/10/good-tools-are-invisible/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Good Tools Are Invisible</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>好工具往往无形</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48858121' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 523</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48858121' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 238</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48858121'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Great tools fade into the background by design, reducing cognitive load and letting people focus on what matters. The piece cautions that invisibility should not hide value from teams and argues for clear outcomes and good integration.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>优秀的工具被设计为在工作流中隐形，降低认知负担，让人专注于核心任务。文章提醒不要让它们的价值被忽视，并强调要有清晰的成效与良好的集成。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48977711' data-tags='Privacy,Politics,Legal,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48977711' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(175) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://edri.org/our-work/the-eu-is-about-to-sell-our-most-sensitive-data-to-the-us-for-visa-free-travel/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The EU is about to sell our most sensitive data to the US for visa-free travel</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>欧盟将把最敏感数据共享给美国以实现免签旅行</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48977711' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 521</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48977711' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 318</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48977711'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>European authorities are moving toward sharing sensitive traveler data with the United States to enable visa-free travel. The plan raises privacy, data protection, and oversight concerns about transatlantic data flows and potential surveillance risks. It highlights tensions between security goals and GDPR-era protections, and may push regulators to tighten controls on data access and purpose limitation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>欧盟拟向美国共享敏感的旅行者数据以实现免签措施，这引发对隐私、数据保护和跨境数据流的担忧。相关安排可能加剧对监控与数据使用边界的质疑，并促使监管机构就数据访问权限和用途限制加强把关。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://edri.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/2026_1Artboard-7.png' data-full='https://edri.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/2026_1Artboard-7.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48786838' data-tags='Legal,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48786838' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(177) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://software.annas-archive.gl/AnnaArchivist/annas-archive/-/work_items/234' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Google Books (or similar) all book scans – $200k bounty (2025)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>谷歌图书（或同类）对完整书籍扫描的20万美元悬赏（2025）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48786838' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 520</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48786838' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 332</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48786838'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A $200k bounty aims to incentivize scanning of an entire book corpus, similar to Google Books. This initiative could accelerate digitization, OCR accuracy, and AI training data availability, while raising licensing, copyright, and provenance questions. Expect debates over public-domain scope, fair use, and the balance between accessibility and rights.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一项价值20万美元的悬赏旨在推动对整套书籍进行数字化扫描，这类似于谷歌图书的计划。此举可能加速书籍的数字化、光学字符识别的准确性，以及可供机器学习训练的数据集，但也引发版权、授权与来源等问题。公众领域、使用权与可获取性的平衡将成为讨论焦点。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://software.annas-archive.gl/assets/twitter_card-570ddb06edf56a2312253c5872489847a0f385112ddbcd71ccfa1570febab5d2.jpg' data-full='https://software.annas-archive.gl/assets/twitter_card-570ddb06edf56a2312253c5872489847a0f385112ddbcd71ccfa1570febab5d2.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48761118' data-tags='Career'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48761118' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(178) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://pradyuprasad.com/writings/how-to-ask-for-help/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How to ask for help from people who don&#x27;t know you</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>如何向不认识你的人寻求帮助</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48761118' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 516</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48761118' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 79</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Career'>Career</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48761118'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Ask for help from people who do not know you can work if you build credibility quickly, keep requests concise, and offer value or an easy path to say yes. The guidance emphasizes clear, respectful asks and the strategic use of warm introductions to overcome friction.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>向陌生人求助往往需要先建立可信度、给出明确的请求并提供回报价值。文章给出一系列实用策略：简短有力的表述、附带背景信息、以及便于对方快速答复的具体问题。把对方从“陌生人”变成愿意帮助的对象，核心在于高效沟通和善用引介。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://pradyuprasad.com/card.png' data-full='https://pradyuprasad.com/card.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48908178' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48908178' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(179) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.artfish.ai/p/offloading-thinking-to-ai' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Are we offloading too much of our thinking to AI?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我们是不是把太多思考交给 AI？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48908178' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 516</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48908178' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 474</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48908178'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>We&#x27;re increasingly offloading thinking to AI, but overreliance risks dulling critical thinking, misframing problems, and eroding human agency. The piece calls for balancing automation with human judgment.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>我们越来越多地把思考交给 AI，文章认为过度依赖会削弱批判性思维、使问题界定偏离实际，并侵蚀个人主动性。应在自动化与人类判断之间保持平衡。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!NTK9!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2Ffb548edd-f827-4d19-aa23-3bd68fdc980c_1456x1092.webp' data-full='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!NTK9!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2Ffb548edd-f827-4d19-aa23-3bd68fdc980c_1456x1092.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48892512' data-tags='Privacy,Security,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48892512' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(181) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://twitter.com/a_green_being/status/2076598897779020159' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Grok uploaded my user directory to xAI&#x27;s servers</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Grok 将我的用户目录上传至 xAI 服务器</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48892512' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 511</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48892512' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 19</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48892512'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A user reports that Grok uploaded their entire user directory to xAI&#x27;s servers, exposing sensitive data and raising privacy concerns around AI tooling. The incident highlights the data-guardrails gap in developer workflows and the need for stricter data handling and disclosure practices.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>用户声称 Grok 将其整个用户目录上传至 xAI 的服务器，暴露敏感数据并引发对 AI 工具中数据隐私的担忧。此事暴露了开发工作流中对数据保护的不足，强调需要更严格的数据处理与披露规范。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://pbs.twimg.com/media/HNGOF9QXwAAU_jp.png:large' data-full='https://pbs.twimg.com/media/HNGOF9QXwAAU_jp.png:large' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48821576' data-tags='AI,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48821576' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(182) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://ariya.io/2026/03/local-cpu-friendly-high-quality-tts-text-to-speech-with-kokoro/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Local, CPU-Friendly, High-Quality TTS (Text-to-Speech) with Kokoro</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>本地化、CPU友好且高质量的 Kokoro TTS</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48821576' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 510</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48821576' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 95</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48821576'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A local, CPU-friendly TTS approach using Kokoro enables high-quality speech synthesis on consumer hardware without relying on cloud services. It highlights a shift toward private, on-device AI that scales with CPUs rather than GPUs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在本地使用 Kokoro 的高质量文本转语音实现，无需云端支持即可在普通硬件上完成语音合成。凸显了将 AI 放在设备端、以 CPU 为主的机会，提升隐私与可离线工作的可能性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://ariya.io//images/2026/03/kokoro.png' data-full='https://ariya.io//images/2026/03/kokoro.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48816959' data-tags='Science,Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48816959' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(183) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://whynothugo.nl/journal/2026/07/03/98-isnt-very-much/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>98% isn&#x27;t much</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>98% 并不算多</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48816959' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 510</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48816959' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 334</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48816959'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A cautionary take on high percentages: 98% can be misleading if base rates, sample quality, or failure modes aren&#x27;t accounted for. The piece argues for richer metrics and stress testing to avoid overestimating performance in real-world use.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>高百分比并不总是等于高可靠性。98% 这样的数字若忽略基线、样本偏差或边缘案例，容易带来错误解读。建议采用更全面的指标和鲁棒性测试来评估真实世界表现。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48933149' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48933149' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(184) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://rtfeldman.com/rust-to-zig' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How Our Rust-to-Zig Rewrite Is Going</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我们的 Rust 转 Zig 重写进展</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48933149' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 507</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48933149' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 269</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48933149'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Progress update on rewriting a Rust codebase in Zig, detailing early results, design choices, and remaining hurdles. It outlines potential gains in memory safety, binary size, and cross-platform portability while acknowledging migration complexity.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文更新了将 Rust 代码库改写为 Zig 的进展，介绍关键设计取舍、初步成果与尚待解决的挑战。讨论了在内存安全、二进制大小和跨平台可移植性方面的潜在收益，同时也强调迁移的复杂性。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(187) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://box2d.org/posts/2026/06/announcing-box3d/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Box3D, an open source 3D physics engine</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>宣布发布 Box3D：开源的 3D 物理引擎</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48745445' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 499</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48745445' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 118</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48745445'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Box3D arrives as an open-source successor in 3D physics engines, following Box2D lineage. It aims to deliver robust rigid-body dynamics with a modular design and easier integration for games and simulations, inviting community contributions. If you build 3D physics into your project, Box3D could become a go-to option without licensing friction.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Box3D 作为 3D 物理引擎的开源继任者加入，沿袭 Box2D 的传统，目标是提供健壮的刚体动力学、模块化设计以及更易的集成，鼓励社区贡献。若你的项目需要 3D 物理，这个引擎可能成为无许可负担的首选选项。</p>

The launch signals Xiaomi&#x27;s push into both consumer and service robotics, aiming to build a broader, more capable robotics stack.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>小米推出全新机器人平台 Xiaomi Robotics-1，标志着公司在消费与服务型机器人领域的扩张。该平台强调模块化硬件、感知与导航能力，以及面向开发者的生态工具。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48734234' data-tags='Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48734234' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(190) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://libertystreeteconomics.newyorkfed.org/2026/06/the-post-covid-decline-in-the-labor-share/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The labor share of income in the US is at its lowest post-war level</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>美国劳动收入份额降至战后最低水平</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48734234' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 498</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48734234' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 540</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48734234'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>US labor’s share of income has declined to a postwar low, signaling that productivity gains and capital income are capturing a growing slice of economic output. The trend implies slower wage growth for workers and potential pressure on consumer demand, with implications for inequality and policy debates. Analysts see a shift in income distribution driven by automation, globalization, and pricing power.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>美国劳动者在国民总收入中的份额降至战后最低水平，反映生产率增益和资本收入正在分得更大份额。此趋势意味着劳动者工资增长可能放缓，消费力受压，且可能加剧收入不平等。分析师将关注结构性因素如何影响工资与投资回报的长期分配。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://libertystreeteconomics.newyorkfed.org/wp-content/uploads/sites/2/2026/06/LSE_2026_labor-share_schuh_460.jpg' data-full='https://libertystreeteconomics.newyorkfed.org/wp-content/uploads/sites/2/2026/06/LSE_2026_labor-share_schuh_460.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48761229' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(192) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.thebignewsletter.com/p/crime-pays-the-egg-bandits-made-a' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Egg Bandits Made a Thousand Times the Fine They Just Paid for Price Fixing</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>蛋价共谋者缴纳的罚款远不及其收益</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48761229' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 491</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48761229' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 246</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48761229'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Fines for egg-price fixers are typically dwarfed by the profits they earned from the scheme. This piece argues for tougher penalties, and possibly criminal charges, to deter cartels and protect consumers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>蛋价垄断案中，罚款往往只是短期成本，远低于参与者从价格操纵中获得的长期收益。文章强调只有提高处罚力度、甚至引入刑事责任，才能真正抑制此类共谋。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Kimi unveils K3-256k, a model with extended context length that can handle longer code and reasoning sessions. The move signals a push toward more capable AI coding assistants and longer, more coherent interactions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Kimi 发布 K3-256k 模型，具备更长的上下文容量，适合处理更长的代码和推理对话。这反映出在代码助手领域追求更强大的对话连贯性和可处理任务的趋势。</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>FAANG 模拟器</p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48836778'>

By exposing candidates to typical formats and questions, it shortens the learning curve and boosts confidence.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这款工具旨在模拟 FAANG 风格的面试，提供题目、打分与反馈，帮助候选人系统备考。通过重复练习常见题型，降低上岸门槛，提升自信与表现。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48983382' data-tags='AI,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48983382' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(195) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://xenaproject.wordpress.com/2026/07/20/human-mathematicians-are-being-outcounterexampled/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Human mathematicians are being outcounterexampled</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>人类数学家正被反例发现所超越</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48983382' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 486</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48983382' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 249</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48983382'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>AI systems are increasingly able to produce counterexamples to mathematical conjectures, sometimes outperforming humans at this specific task. This shift reshapes collaboration between mathematicians and machines, emphasizing verification, reproducibility, and new roles for intuition in research. The trend could accelerate discovery but also demands careful framing of proofs and trust in automated reasoning.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>人工智能系统日益擅长发现数学猜想的反例，有时在这个特定任务上甚至优于人类。这种变化正在重新定义数学家与机器之间的协作，强调验证、可重复性，以及直觉在研究中的新角色。此趋势可能加速发现，但也需要对证明与自动推理的信任进行审慎权衡。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://xenaproject.wordpress.com/wp-content/uploads/2020/05/cropped-twitchpfc.png?w=200' data-full='https://xenaproject.wordpress.com/wp-content/uploads/2020/05/cropped-twitchpfc.png?w=200' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48883343' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48883343' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(196) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://geohot.github.io//blog/jekyll/update/2026/07/12/i-love-llms.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I love LLMs, I hate hype</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我爱大语言模型，也讨厌炒作</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48883343' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 486</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48883343' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 318</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48883343'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A measured take on LLMs, acknowledging their capabilities while warning against hype, and urging careful evaluation, realistic expectations, and responsible deployment.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对大语言模型保持审慎态度，既肯定其能力，又批评炒作，倡导理性评估、务实期望和负责任的落地应用。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48784777' data-tags='DevOps,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48784777' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(197) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://peteris.rocks/blog/htop/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Explanation of everything you can see in htop/top on Linux (2019)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Linux 中 htop/top 的全部字段解读</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48784777' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 485</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48784777' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 61</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48784777'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A comprehensive guide decodes every field in htop and top on Linux, explaining what each metric means and how to interpret them in practice. It helps practitioners diagnose performance bottlenecks beyond surface readings. Includes tips on CPU/memory usage, process states, and how sampling affects observations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇详尽指南逐一解释 Linux 上 htop/top 的每个字段和指标，以及如何据此解读性能信号、定位瓶颈。提供实用建议，帮助读者理解 CPU/内存、进程状态和 I/O 等数据在监控中的含义及刷新对观察的影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://peteris.rocks/blog/htop/canyoukillit-before.png' data-full='https://peteris.rocks/blog/htop/canyoukillit-before.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48832212' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48832212' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(198) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://mistral.ai/news/robostral-navigate/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Mistral&#x27;s Robostral Navigate: a state of the art robotics navigation model</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Mistral 的 Robostral Navigate：前沿机器人导航模型</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48832212' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 485</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48832212' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 110</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48832212'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Mistral unveils Robostral Navigate, a state-of-the-art robotics navigation model designed to improve real-time planning, obstacle avoidance, and multi-sensor fusion. Early demonstrations suggest stronger robustness and efficiency in dynamic environments, with broad potential for logistics and service robotics.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Mistral 发布 Robostral Navigate，这是一款前沿的机器人导航模型，聚焦实时规划、避障和多传感器融合。早期演示显示在动态环境中具备更强鲁棒性与效率，未来可能推动物流、服务机器人等领域。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://mistral.ai/cms-media/api/media/file/Robostral-navigate.jpg' data-full='https://mistral.ai/cms-media/api/media/file/Robostral-navigate.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49007247' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49007247' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(199) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://resobscura.substack.com/p/quality-non-fiction-books-are-the' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Quality non-fiction books are the antithesis of AI slop</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>高质量非虚构书籍是 AI 俗作文的对立面</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49007247' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 483</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49007247' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 235</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49007247'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A pointed critique arguing that well-researched, carefully written non-fiction remains essential in a world chasing AI-generated content. The piece emphasizes rigor, citations, and nuance as defining traits that distinguish quality writing from AI churn.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇尖锐的评论认为经过充分研究、精心撰写的非虚构作品在追逐 AI 内容的时代依然至关重要。作者强调严格性、引文与语义深度，指出这些是区分高质量写作与 AI 产出差异的关键。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48947825' data-tags='AI,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48947825' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(200) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://stateofopensource.ai/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The state of open source AI</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Mozilla：开源 AI 的现状</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48947825' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 479</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48947825' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 351</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48947825'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Open-source AI platforms are maturing, with new models, licensing debates, and governance challenges shaping the ecosystem. The piece benchmarks how open tooling stacks up against proprietary options and why safety, transparency, and community norms matter for long-term viability.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>开源 AI 正在走向成熟，新的模型、许可与治理挑战共同塑造生态。相较于商业垄断，开放工具链在安全性、透明度和社区规范方面的表现将决定其长期可用性与影响力。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://stateofopensource.ai/assets/img/moz-logo.png' data-full='https://stateofopensource.ai/assets/img/moz-logo.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
